Further complicating community moderation are norms that center anti-censorship and non-judgment, which leave moderators to justify their actions using the limits of the law and terms of service. We end by reflecting on the ways in which technical, community, and legal governance may most effectively mitigate the production of abusive content.

</details>


### [182] [Weird Machines in Transport Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13685)
*Michael Collins,Jada Cumberland,Brianne Dunn,Ross Gore,Samuel Jackson,Sachin Shetty,Jonathan Takeshita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将怪癖机器（weird machine）理论扩展到TLS握手协议及其两个主要实现（OpenSSL和BoringSSL），证明合法的TLS原语（会话缓存、重协商逻辑、扩展解析、证书验证）可以组合成图灵完备系统，并通过"信任驱动"（trust actuation）将计算与认证决策耦合。作者构建了两个基于真实OpenSSL代码路径的演示：一个防御性的哨兵系统和一种认证绕过攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已在软件系统（x86指令、ELF元数据、页表）和网络物理系统（工业控制网络）中研究了怪癖机器现象，但尚未将这一理论系统地应用到TLS握手协议领域。作者希望拓展该理论的应用边界，并揭示TLS实现中合法原语组合可能产生的未被充分认知的计算能力。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了"信任驱动"（trust actuation）这一概念，论证任何提供会话存储、序列计数器算术、握手状态条件分支以及恢复/重试循环迭代的TLS实现都满足任意计算条件。随后在OpenSSL真实代码路径上构建两个演示系统：（1）哨兵系统——组合标准TLS原语形成防御机制；（2）认证绕过——通过中途连接重协商击败密码强度策略检查，无需内存破坏或外部恶意软件。

Result: 两个演示均在真实服务器和客户端二进制（Docker环境）上成功运行。第一个展示了TLS原语组合可用于构建防御性异常检测机制；第二个展示了一种实际的认证绕过攻击，通过重协商绕过密码强度策略检查，且不依赖任何内存破坏或外部恶意软件。

Conclusion: TLS握手协议中存在可被构造为图灵完备系统的怪癖机器，其计算通过"信任驱动"与认证和信任决策耦合而非物理驱动。这为TLS安全分析提供了新的理论框架，同时也揭示了仅依赖标准TLS功能组合（而非利用内存漏洞）即可实现认证绕过的潜在攻击面。

Abstract: Weird machines are latent computational capabilities that emerge from the composition of architectural components. Prior work has studied this phenomenon extensively in software systems, including x86 instructions, ELF metadata, and page tables, and more recently in cyber-physical systems such as industrial control networks. This paper extends weird machine theory to a new domain: the Transport Layer Security (TLS) handshake and its two dominant implementations, OpenSSL and BoringSSL.
  We show that legitimate TLS primitives, including session cache entries, renegotiation logic, extension parsing, and certificate verification steps, compose into Turing-complete systems whose computation is coupled to authentication and trust decisions rather than physical actuation. We formalize this coupling, which we call trust actuation, and argue that any TLS implementation providing session storage, arithmetic on sequence counters, conditional branching on handshake state, and iteration through resumption or retry loops satisfies the conditions for arbitrary computation.
  We validate this theory with two working demonstrations built on real OpenSSL code paths. The first, a sentinel system, composes standard TLS primitives into a defensive mechanism that detects anomalous handshake behavior. The second, an authentication bypass, composes the same class of primitives into an attack that defeats a cipher-strength policy check through mid-connection renegotiation, without any memory corruption or external malware. Both demonstrations run against real server and client binaries in Docker.

</details>


### [183] [A Reproducibility Protocol for Cross-Implementation Evaluation of Post-Quantum ACVP Test Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13784)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究定义了产品中立的可复现协议（v2），对三种公开实现（@noble/post-quantum 0.7.0、liboqs 0.16.0、Go 1.26.4）的NIST ML-KEM进行了对照NIST验证明文语料库的系统性复现实验。2160条基础记录完全符合预期：1650条可执行评估全部匹配NIST预言机，510条不支持记录匹配Go的预声明能力边界。两两配对的可执行重叠部分完全一致（noble-liboqs 720/720，Go配对各210/210）。但作者强调这仅证明有界的作者运行可复现性，不构成认证、穷尽正确性、侧信道抵抗、安全集成或生产保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学标准独立实现应当产生相同的已知答案（KAT）结果，但只有当语料库、修订版本、公共接口、排除项和证据都被精确说明时，实现间的一致性才有意义。现有的ML-KEM实现缺乏统一的复现协议，导致"一致性"这一概念难以被严格验证。

Method: 定义产品中立复现协议v2：固定供应商特定能力、统一应用验证错误分类法、保留每个选定用例、分离字节/验证结论/不支持操作/适配器错误。对三种实现（noble、liboqs、Go）进行源构建实验，针对固定的公共ACVP语料库运行完整笛卡尔积，共2160条基础记录，并设计了三个冻结控制（字节比较、结论比较、畸形响应错误分离）及keyGen-ek投影诊断。

Result: 1650条声明为可执行的评估记录全部与NIST预言机匹配；510条不支持记录全部匹配Go的能力边界。可执行重叠部分两两完全一致（noble-liboqs 720/720；Go与另两者各210/210）。keyGen-ek投影诊断匹配全部150条Go封装密钥投影。三个冻结控制均产生预设结果，无基础失败、适配器错误或状态不稳定。

Conclusion: 实验确证了有界的作者运行可复现性，但暴露了实际标准缺口：公开ML-KEM包提供了实质不同的确定性和验证测试表面。独立外部复现尚未观测。结果不构成认证、穷尽正确性、侧信道抵抗、安全集成或生产保证。

Abstract: Independent implementations of a cryptographic standard should reproduce the same known-answer results, yet agreement is meaningful only when the corpus, revisions, public interfaces, exclusions, and evidence are precisely stated. This study defines a product-neutral reproducibility protocol for three public implementations of NIST ML-KEM against a pinned public Automated Cryptographic Validation Protocol corpus. Protocol v2 freezes provider-specific capabilities, applies one validation-error taxonomy symmetrically, preserves every selected case, and separates bytes, validation verdicts, unsupported operations, and adapter errors. The source-built experiment evaluated @noble/post-quantum 0.7.0, liboqs 0.16.0, and Go 1.26.4. Across three repetitions, the required Cartesian product comprised 2,160 base records: all 1,650 declared executable evaluations matched the NIST oracle, and all 510 unsupported records matched Go's predeclared capability boundary. Pairwise agreement was complete on every executable overlap: 720 of 720 noble-liboqs records and 210 of 210 records for each Go pairing. A separate keyGen-ek-projection diagnostic matched all 150 Go encapsulation-key projections without counting them as full key generation. Three frozen controls independently exercised byte comparison, verdict comparison, and malformed-response error separation; each produced its exact predeclared outcome. No base failure, adapter error, or status instability occurred. The evidence establishes bounded author-run repeatability and exposes a practical standards gap: public ML-KEM packages provide materially different deterministic and validation-test surfaces. Independent external reproduction remains unobserved. The results do not establish certification, exhaustive correctness, side-channel resistance, secure integration, or production assurance.

</details>


### [184] [The ack3 H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset: Audit Scope Across 135 Security Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13792)
*Josef Gattermayer,Jan Kalivoda,Arman Bašović*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于H1 2026 DeFi事件数据集（含135个安全事件、9.3986亿美元损失），分析了68个可追溯审计历史的案例，发现67.6%（按数量）和94.4%（按损失）的攻击路径不在任何审计范围内，表明"已审计"标签常被过度引申为对整个项目的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约审计通常只覆盖特定时间点的特定工件，但"已审计"标签常被市场误读为项目级全面保障。现有研究缺乏大规模实证数据来量化审计范围与实际攻击路径之间的错配程度。

Method: 使用Ack3发布的高频DeFi事件数据集，对135起安全事件的审计历史与攻击路径范围进行交叉分析；分类为路径在范围内/范围外/未决，并按数量和损失金额进行加权统计，同时进行排除极值的敏感性分析。

Result: 68起可追溯审计历史的事件中，46条攻击路径在所有公开审计范围之外，20条在至少一个范围内，2条未决；范围外路径占事件数67.6%、占损失额94.4%。剔除两个大额事件后，损失占比降至72.1%，但结论方向不变。

Conclusion: 项目级审计历史与事件路径审计范围是两个不同的变量；"已审计"不应被解读为项目整体保障，审计覆盖范围与实际攻击路径之间的鸿沟应成为行业关注重点。

Abstract: Smart-contract audits cover defined artifacts at a specific time, but the label audited is often treated as project-wide assurance. We analyze audit history and incident-path scope across 135 DeFi security incidents using the H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset published by cybersecurity company ack3 (https://ack3.ai), covering 1 January to 29 June 2026. The corpus reports USD 939.86 million in attributed loss. Audit history was identified for 68 incidents. Of these, 46 attack paths were outside all identified public pre-incident audit scopes, 20 were inside at least one scope, and 2 were unresolved. Within this 68-incident subset, outside-scope paths represented 67.6% by count and 94.4% of reported loss. The loss-weighted result was concentrated in two large incidents; excluding both reduced the share to 72.1%, while preserving the direction of the result. We also describe audit age, temporal loss distribution, and affected project types. The results show that project-level audit history and incident-path scope are distinct variables.

</details>


### [185] [Mobile Apps vs. Web Browsers: A User Perception Study with Android Apps and Google Chrome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13803)
*Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验和问卷调查，探讨Android智能手机用户在访问在线服务时，对移动应用（App）与网页浏览器在安全性、隐私性和可用性方面的感知差异，并为开发者改进两个平台提供建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在选择移动App还是网页浏览器访问在线服务时，其安全、隐私和可用性方面的感知和偏好尚不明确，这影响了用户的使用决策，也制约了开发者对两个平台的针对性改进。

Method: 采用实验（experiment）和问卷调查（survey）相结合的研究方法，以Android智能手机用户为研究对象，收集并分析用户对移动应用与网页浏览器在安全、隐私和可用性方面的感知数据。

Result: 研究识别了影响用户在移动App与网页浏览器之间选择的关键关注点和偏好，揭示了用户在安全性、隐私性和可用性方面的主要认知与误解。

Conclusion: 研究结论可为开发者提供改进移动App和网页浏览器安全性、隐私性及可用性的实践指导，通过解决用户的实际关切和误解来优化用户体验。

Abstract: This study examines user perceptions of mobile applications (apps) versus web browsers for accessing online services, with an emphasis on security, privacy, and usability aspects. Through a combination of an experiment and a survey with Android smartphone users, the research seeks to identify the key concerns and preferences that influence their choice between mobile apps and web browsers. The findings will offer valuable insights for developers to improve the security, privacy, and usability of both platforms by addressing user concerns and misconceptions.

</details>


### [186] [Characterizing the Variance Envelope: A Multi-Dimensional Analysis of Spectre Telemetry Across Architectures and Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13920)
*Jaya Keshava Chandra Kotha,Jean-Luc Gaudiot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地刻画了硬件攻击（如Spectre）在不同架构（Intel、ARM、AMD）、攻击变体、流量节流模式和后台噪声条件下的HPC签名"方差包络"，证明静态检测模型在真实环境中不可靠，并提出需要架构感知的自适应监测方案。同时揭示了AMD Jaguar上Prime+Probe攻击存在持续性的微架构级硬件失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的HPC攻击检测在受控环境中表现良好，但静态模型在面对真实世界的后台系统噪声、多样化攻击变体和对抗性流量节流时效率低下。现有研究缺少对攻击签名在不同架构和环境条件下如何漂移的系统性刻画，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 论文定义并刻画了"方差包络"，即攻击签名变化的全范围。作者构建了包含三种攻击变体、四种流量节流模式和四种后台噪声条件的大规模实验矩阵，在Intel、ARM和AMD三种架构上进行系统评估，分析HPC指标的脆弱性。

Result: 分析证明HPC签名高度脆弱，极易被执行环境扭曲。此外，实验揭示了AMD Jaguar上Prime+Probe攻击存在持续性的微架构级硬件失败，这是一个关键的硬件瓶颈。整体上，静态模型在不同环境和架构下无法维持可靠的检测性能。

Conclusion: 可靠的运行时攻击检测需要架构感知的自适应监测方案，而非依赖静态模型。研究结果强调了在真实部署环境中，检测系统必须能够适应不同硬件平台和动态执行条件的差异。

Abstract: Hardware attacks like Spectre exploit built-in processor vulnerabilities, leaving anomalous footprints in Hardware Performance Counter (HPC) metrics. While machine learning can detect these footprints in controlled settings, static models fail in the real world when confronted with background system noise, diverse attack variants, and adversarial traffic pacing. To close this gap, this paper characterizes the "variance envelope"-the full range of how attack signatures shift- across Intel, ARM, and AMD architectures. We evaluate an extensive experimental matrix encompassing three attack variants, four pacing modes, and four background-noise conditions. Our analysis proves that HPC signatures are highly fragile and easily warped by their execution environment. Furthermore, we expose a critical microarchitectural bottleneck: the persistent, hardware-level failure of Prime+Probe attacks on the AMD Jaguar. Ultimately, this comprehensive characterization demonstrates that reliable runtime detection requires architecture-aware, adaptive monitoring rather than static models.

</details>


### [187] [Vaulted Passkeys: A Device-Bound Proposal for Authenticated Credential Export and Import](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13806)
*Pol Henarejos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Vaulted Passkeys架构，通过受保护的凭证状态导出/导入机制解决硬件认证器在替换、灾难恢复和受控迁移场景下的恢复缺口，在不克隆完整认证器且不向桌面软件暴露明文私钥的前提下实现凭证的受控迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件认证器刻意抵抗私钥提取，但替换、灾难恢复和受控迁移产生了合理的可移植性需求。现有指南通过预先注册额外认证器来降低恢复风险，但这只是冗余而非原始凭证的备份，需要在故障前拥有替代硬件并创建独立的凭证注册，存在恢复缺口。

Method: 提出Vaulted Passkeys设备绑定架构：使用随机256位Kvault密钥保护认证的PKV1凭证信封，通过HKDF分离密钥和四种显式AEAD配置文件实现保护。设计将注册与导出/导入分离，将必需的vault与可选的identity分离，并贡献了角色分离的系统模型、线格式、威胁分析、实现映射和可证伪的评估计划。

Result: 原型验证了方案的可行性，但作者明确承认该原型既非形式化安全证明，也非提议的最终标准。

Conclusion: Vaulted Passkeys填补了现有设备绑定凭证冗余策略与真正凭证备份之间的恢复缺口，实现了在源可用时导出受保护的凭证状态并在后续硬件上恢复，同时避免克隆完整认证器或向桌面软件暴露明文私钥。

Abstract: Hardware authenticators deliberately resist private-key extraction, yet replacement, disaster recovery, and controlled migration create a legitimate need for portability. Existing guidance for device-bound credentials commonly reduces recovery risk by registering an additional authenticator before failure. That creates an independent credential registration and requires replacement hardware to exist in advance; it is redundancy, not a backup of the original credential. This paper addresses the resulting recovery gap by exporting protected credential state while the source is available and restoring it to hardware acquired later, without cloning a complete authenticator or exposing plaintext private keys to routine desktop software. We propose Vaulted Passkeys, a device-bound architecture in which a random 256-bit Kvault protects authenticated PKV1 credential envelopes through HKDF-separated keys and four explicit AEAD profiles. The design separates enrollment from export/import and the required vault from optional identity. We contribute a role-separated system model, wire format, threat analysis, implementation mapping, and falsifiable evaluation plan. The prototype demonstrates feasibility but is neither a formal security proof nor a proposed final standard.

</details>


### [188] [TLF: Rapid Characterization of RF Transceiver Parameters in Embedded Systems via Bus-Level Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13815)
*Larry Hernandez,Sergey Bratus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TLF（收发器提升框架）是一种从微控制器与收发器IC之间的总线级痕迹中恢复RF收发器配置和运行时行为的工具。它通过有状态的协议解码器重建调制、频段规划、相位行为等RF参数，并能提取跳频序列、信道表和硬件密钥材料。在SX1233和SX1276两个不同Semtech家族的平台上验证，可在数秒内恢复完整RF配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着RF设备（如UAV遥控链路、LoRa节点）的广泛应用，能够从硬件层面逆向分析其收发器配置和运行行为对安全评估具有重要意义。现有的分析手段通常需要固件知识或专用硬件，缺乏一种无需先验固件知识即可从低层总线痕迹中系统性恢复RF参数的通用方法。

Method: TLF构建了一个针对收发器寄存器与数据接口的有状态协议解码器，从微控制器与收发器IC之间的总线迹跟踪中拦截寄存器写入和FIFO传输，重建RF运行参数和行为。对于硬件辅助跳频引擎或自定义跳频调度，解码器可提取信道表、跳频序列和时间节奏。此外提供可插拔的协议层解码器，将恢复的FIFO负载解析为应用PDU。

Result: 在两个目标平台上进行了评估：基于SX1233的UAV C2调制解调器（采用固件级FHSS和逐包同步字旋转）和基于SX1276的Meshtastic节点（使用LoRa寄存器覆盖层）。单次总线捕获在数秒内即可恢复完整的寄存器暴露RF配置（调制、频段规划、相位行为），无需先验固件知识；在Meshtastic上实现了端到端应用PDU解析。

Conclusion: TLF证明了从单次总线捕获即可恢复收发器的完整RF配置以配置匹配接收器或开发针对性对抗措施。恢复深度受限于收发器寄存器接口——完全在固件中实现的参数（如自定义FEC、白化、加密）只能作为不透明的FIFO负载被观测。该方法需要物理访问或目标硬件仿真。

Abstract: We present TLF (Transceiver Lifter Framework), a tool for recovering RF transceiver configuration and runtime behavior from bus-level traces captured between a microcontroller and its transceiver IC. A stateful protocol decoder, built against the transceiver's register and data interface, reconstructs operating RF parameters and behavior from intercepted register writes and FIFO transfers. For bus-attached transceivers whose hardware-cryptography keys are loaded through the intercepted host interface, key material is also recoverable. Where the firmware drives frequency hopping -- either through a hardware-assisted engine or a custom schedule -- the decoder extracts the channel table, hop sequence, and timing. We evaluate the approach on two targets from different Semtech families: an SX1233-based UAV C2 modem employing firmware-level FHSS with per-packet sync word rotation, and an SX1276-based Meshtastic node exercising the LoRa register overlay. From a single bus capture, processed in seconds, TLF recovers the complete register-exposed RF configuration (modulation, band plan, phase behavior) without prior knowledge of the target firmware -- sufficient to configure a matched receiver or develop targeted countermeasures. Above the chip layer, a pluggable protocol decoder interprets recovered FIFO payloads as application PDUs, demonstrated end-to-end on Meshtastic. Firmware-level cryptographic state remains, as expected, opaque. The approach requires physical access or emulation of the target hardware, and its recovery depth is bounded by the transceiver's register interface: parameters implemented entirely in firmware (custom FEC, whitening, encryption) are observable only as opaque FIFO payloads.

</details>


### [189] [Verified Pythagorean Composition for Adaptive Cryptographic Games: Noise Flooding in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13846)
*Yi Lee,Alexandru Cojocaru,Junyi Liu,Xiaodi Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文使用Rocq和SSProve对近似同态加密中噪声洪泛（noise flooding）抗解密攻击安全性的参数关键性证明进行了机械化验证，通过构建新的关系程序逻辑（Pythagorean判断），将条件KL代价逐次组合并在最终一次性转换为统计距离，形式化证明了q查询IND-CPAD安全性约简。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声洪泛是对近似同态加密解密攻击的标准防御手段，但其安全证明对组合（composition）异常敏感。朴素混合论证在q次适应性解密应答中会线性损失安全性，而密码学证明需要累积条件KL代价后一次性转换，以获得参数关键的平方根损失。这一论证的微妙性迫切需要机械化验证来确保正确性。

Method: 使用Rocq和SSProve对论证进行机器检查；在SSProve语义上构建新的关系程序逻辑，其Pythagorean判断支持条件KL预算的组合而不转换为统计距离；通过一个验证过的trace编译器将局部oracle规则提升到任意自适应程序，仅在最终阶段做一次转换。

Result: 对任意近似正确且IND-CPA安全的FHE方案，形式化证明了每个q查询IND-CPAD敌手的约简不等式：Pr[IND-CPAD_NF^A=1] ≤ β_CPA(B_{A,q}) + √(qn)/(2γ)，其中n为明文维度，γ为洪泛宽度乘子。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了噪声洪泛安全证明机械化验证的空白，其Pythagorean判断逻辑为处理依赖组合的密码学安全证明提供了新的形式化工具，确保了参数关键性（平方根损失）在机器证明中的正确性。

Abstract: Noise flooding is a standard defense against decryption attacks on approximate homomorphic encryption, but its security proof is unusually sensitive to composition. Replacing each of $q$ adaptive decryption answers with a statistically close simulation and applying an ordinary hybrid argument loses linearly in $q$. The cryptographic proof instead accumulates conditional Kullback-Leibler (KL) costs and converts to statistical distance once, giving the parameter-critical square-root loss.
  We machine-check this argument using Rocq and SSProve. Given any fully homomorphic encryption scheme that is approximately correct and IND-CPA secure, we formalize a reduction for every $q$-query IND-CPAD adversary and prove \[
  \Pr[\mathsf{IND\text{-}CPAD}_{\mathsf{NF}}^{\mathcal A}=1]
  \leq β_{\mathsf{CPA}}(\mathcal B_{\mathcal A,q})
  + \frac{\sqrt{qn}}{2γ}. \] where $n$ is the plaintext dimension and $γ$ is the flooding-width multiplier. Our proof constructs a new relational program logic over SSProve semantics. Its Pythagorean judgment composes conditional KL budgets without converting them to statistical distance, and a verified trace compiler lifts a local oracle rule to arbitrary adaptive programs with a single final conversion.

</details>


### [190] [CipherSight: Robust Website Fingerprinting via Record-Resource Semantic Supervision under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13905)
*Runhan Song,Qiqi Liu,Chuanzhou Pan,Zhenquan Ding,Youquan Xian,Chongru Fan,Lei Cui,Wei Wang,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了CipherSight，一种基于TLS记录的分层框架，用于解决HTTPS网站指纹识别中的分布外(OOD)问题，在闭世界和开世界场景下均展现出优异的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界部署中HTTPS网站指纹识别面临显著的分布外(OOD)问题，包括由时间和地理变化引起的分布漂移，以及开世界场景中大量未见网站。现有方法主要依赖原始TCP包序列，对传输层伪影敏感，难以捕获稳定且可泛化的网站表示，导致在实际条件下性能严重下降。

Method: CipherSight采用基于TLS记录的分层架构：1) 从TLS记录而非TCP包序列中学习网站表示，联合编码多个记录级属性；2) 分层设计捕获流内（TLS记录间的依赖关系）和流间（并发流之间的交互）两个层面的结构模式；3) 引入掩码记录建模(MRM)任务学习上下文流量语义；4) 利用细粒度的记录-资源注释作为特权监督，通过结构感知目标和语义蒸馏增强表示鲁棒性。

Result: 在包含2000多个网站类别的闭世界设置下达到95.41%的准确率；在时间和地理漂移条件下保持超过90%的准确率，在所有评估的基线方法中持续取得最优性能。

Conclusion: CipherSight证明基于TLS记录的分层建模比基于TCP包序列的方法更能捕获稳定且可泛化的网站表示，能有效应对实际部署中时间和地理漂移带来的分布外挑战，显著提升HTTPS网站指纹识别在真实条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: HTTPS website fingerprinting (WF) aims to identify visited websites from metadata observable in encrypted traffic. However, real-world deployments introduce a significant out-of-distribution (OOD) problem caused by temporal and geographic changes, while previously unseen websites are common in open-world scenarios. Existing methods primarily learn from raw TCP packet sequences and struggle to capture stable and generalizable website representations, resulting in performance degradation under practical conditions.
  We propose CipherSight, a TLS-record-based hierarchical framework for robust HTTPS WF. Unlike existing approaches that rely on TCP packet sequences and are sensitive to transport-layer artifacts, CipherSight learns website representations from TLS records by jointly encoding multiple record-level attributes. It introduces a hierarchical architecture that captures both intra-flow dependencies among TLS records and inter-flow interactions across concurrent flows, enabling the model to exploit structural patterns in HTTPS traffic. Besides, to learn robust representations, CipherSight employs a masked record modeling (MRM) task to capture contextual traffic semantics and leverages fine-grained record-resource annotations as privileged supervision through structure-aware objectives and semantic distillation. Experiments show that CipherSight achieves 95.41% accuracy across more than 2,000 website classes in the closed-world setting and maintains over 90% accuracy under both temporal and geographic drift, consistently outperforming all evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [191] [CoSA: Context-Aware Severity Assessment via Context Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13928)
*Jinfeng Jiang,Yikun Li,Chengran Yang,Ting Zhang,Wen Bin Leow,Yide Yin,Eng Lieh Ouh,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CoSA是一种上下文感知的漏洞严重性评估方法，通过构建代码属性图并结合两阶段仓库剪枝策略（静态剪枝+LLM引导剪枝），从仓库级证据中推断CVSS基础指标，在6,816个标注实例上超越函数级和纯LLM基线，预测准确率提升14.4%，Macro-F1提升15.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动评估CVSS基础指标耗时费力，现有自动化方法难以捕获评估CVSS指标所需的仓库级证据，而仓库相关证据分散在整个仓库中且夹杂大量噪声。

Method: CoSA构建代码属性图（CPG），采用两阶段仓库剪枝策略：先用轻量级静态剪枝保留结构邻近上下文，再用基于智能体LLM的剪枝步骤保留CVSS相关上下文并收集支持性证据。LLM将检索到的仓库上下文整合为紧凑的、按CVSS指标划分的文本摘要，输入轻量级transformer预测器。此外构建了包含6,816个CVSS标注实例、覆盖90种CWE类型的仓库级数据集。

Result: 在真实漏洞上的实验表明，CoSA在预测准确率上提升14.4%，Macro-F1上提升15.3%，持续优于函数级和纯LLM基线。

Conclusion: 显式的、面向指标的仓库上下文检索对于实用可靠的自动化严重性评估至关重要。

Abstract: Accurate vulnerability severity assessment is essential for prioritizing remediation, yet manually assessing Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) base metrics remains labor-intensive. Existing automated approaches often fail to capture the repository-level evidence required for assessing many CVSS base metrics. Such repository-aware assessment is challenging because relevant evidence is scattered across the entire repository under heavy noise.
  To address these challenges, we present CoSA, a Context-aware vulnerability Severity Assessment approach that infers CVSS base metrics from repository artifacts. CoSA constructs a code property graph (CPG) and applies a two-stage repository-pruning strategy: lightweight static pruning to preserve structurally proximal context, followed by an agentic large language model (LLM)-guided pruning step to retain CVSS-relevant context while collecting supporting evidence. The LLM then consolidates the retrieved repository context into compact, CVSS metric-wise textual summaries, which are fed into a lightweight transformer predictor. We also construct a higher-quality repository-level dataset comprising 6,816 CVSS labeled instances spanning 90 Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) types.
  Experiments on real-world vulnerabilities show that CoSA consistently outperforms function-level and pure-LLM baselines. It improves prediction accuracy by 14.4% and Macro-F1 by 15.3% over the best-performing baseline, suggesting that explicit, metric-oriented repository context retrieval is crucial for practical and reliable automated severity assessment.

</details>


### [192] [Extracting and Verifying Illicit Bitcoin Addresses from Underground Forum Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13930)
*Abdoul Nasser Hassane Amadou,Arnaud Legout,Imane Fouad,Konstantin Avrachenkov,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的比特币非法地址标注管线，基于HackForums地下论坛十五年存档，结合LLM辅助筛选、专家审核和链上验证，构建包含2,438个经人工验证的非法比特币地址数据集（2010-2024年），并公开数据集与完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有比特币标注数据集多源于社区举报、区块链启发式规则、事件专项收集或专有标注流程，其构造方法难以公开复现，且缺乏证据表明地址直接参与非法活动。亟需一个可复现、有证据支撑的构建方法。

Method: 搭建可复现管线，包括三阶段：1）LLM辅助筛选，从HackForums论坛讨论中初筛涉及比特币非法交易的地址；2）专家审核，人工验证筛选结果；3）链上验证，确认地址在区块链上确实发生与论坛讨论相关的非法交易。每个标签均附带地下论坛讨论的上下文证据。

Result: 构建的数据集包含2,438个经人工验证的非法比特币地址（覆盖2010-2024年），归入12个网络犯罪类别（由LLM筛选阶段分类），并公开数据集、时间元数据及完整提取管线。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个基于证据、可完全复现的比特币非法地址标注方案，弥补了现有数据集在可复现性和证据充分性方面的不足，支持加密货币相关网络犯罪的再现性研究。

Abstract: Existing labeled Bitcoin datasets are largely derived from community-reported abuse, blockchain heuristics, incident-specific collections, or proprietary labeling processes. Their construction methods are rarely publicly reproducible and often provide limited evidence that an address was directly involved in illicit activity. We present a reproducible pipeline for constructing evidence-backed Bitcoin labels from HackForums, an underground cybercrime forum with fifteen years of archived activity. The pipeline combines LLM-assisted screening, expert review, and on-chain validation to identify Bitcoin addresses explicitly associated with illicit transactions discussed on the forum. Each released label is supported by contextual evidence from underground discussions and validated on-chain. The resulting dataset contains 2,438 manually verified illicit Bitcoin addresses spanning 2010-2024 and twelve cybercrime categories assigned during LLM screening. We release the dataset, temporal metadata, and the complete extraction pipeline to support reproducible research on cryptocurrency-facilitated cybercrime.

</details>


### [193] [Exposing SIMD Parallelism in SQIsign: An AVX-512 Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13948)
*Weize Wang,Chutong Wang,Yu Wu,Qifan Xue,Jieyu Zheng,Yunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文展示了通过重构高级原语的算术依赖图而非孤立地向量化域乘法，可以在基于同源的密码系统中恢复大量SIMD并行性。作者开发了SQIsign的端到端AVX-512IFMA实现，在NIST level I下获得1.76×/1.71×/3.18×的密钥生成/签名/验证加速；结合Qlapoti后升至2.90×/2.69×；并推广到CORAL协议获得1.28–1.40×和1.92–2.46×的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于同源的密码系统（如SQIsign）大部分运行时间花在有限域、椭圆曲线和高维同源运算中。这些运算利用SIMD并行性存在显著困难：Montgomery梯子等例程包含循环携带依赖，而点、配对和theta坐标公式只暴露不规则的细粒度并行性。作者的核心洞察是，通过重组高级原语的算术依赖图而非孤立地向量化域乘法，可以有效地恢复SIMD并行性。

Method: 作者开发了一个端到端的AVX-512IFMA实现SQIsign方案，数据在大部分曲线侧计算中以radix-2^51的向量表示形式保持。关键设计包括：投影xDBLADD调度、多种坐标系下的批量点加倍、向量化的双标量梯子、融合的立方算术配对步骤，以及批量的一维和二维同源求值。此外，将同一后端和方法论应用于CORAL（基于二维2-同源的量子后非交互密钥交换方案）。

Result: 相对于参考C实现，在NIST level I下，密钥生成、签名和验证分别获得1.76×、1.71×和3.18×的加速；与Qlapoti结合后，密钥生成和签名加速提升至2.90×和2.69×。对于CORAL，在五组参数下，密钥生成获得1.28–1.40×的加速，共享密钥计算获得1.92–2.46×的加速。

Conclusion: 这些跨方案的结果提供了证据，表明算法级SIMD调度是高维同源密码学中一个可重用的优化维度，能够在不同基于同源的密码方案间推广应用。

Abstract: Modern isogeny-based cryptosystems spend much of their running time in finite-field, elliptic-curve, and higher-dimensional isogeny arithmetic. Exploiting SIMD parallelism is nontrivial: routines such as Montgomery ladders contain loop-carried dependencies, while point, pairing, and theta-coordinate formulas expose only irregular fine-grained parallelism. We show that substantial SIMD parallelism can be recovered by reorganizing the arithmetic dependency graphs of higher-level primitives rather than vectorizing field multiplication in isolation.
  We develop an end-to-end AVX-512IFMA implementation of SQIsign in which data remain in a radix-$2^{51}$ vector representation across most of the curve-side computation. Our redesign includes projective xDBLADD schedules, batched point doubling in several coordinate systems, a vectorized biscalar ladder, fused cubical-arithmetic pairing steps, and batched one- and two-dimensional isogeny evaluation. Relative to the reference C implementation, we achieve end-to-end speedups of $1.76\times$, $1.71\times$, and $3.18\times$ for key generation, signing, and verification at NIST level~I; combined with Qlapoti, key-generation and signing speedups rise to $2.90\times$ and $2.69\times$.
  We further apply the same backend and methodology to CORAL, a recent isogeny group action for post-quantum non-interactive key exchange based on two-dimensional $2$-isogenies. Across five parameter sets, this yields $1.28$--$1.40\times$ speedups for key generation and $1.92$--$2.46\times$ for shared-key computation. These results provide cross-scheme evidence that algorithm-level SIMD scheduling is a reusable optimization dimension for higher-dimensional isogeny cryptography.

</details>


### [194] [Structural Leakage in Graph Encryption: Attacks and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13981)
*Hua Shen,Renzhi Chen,Ge Wu,Willy Susilo,Jing Chen,Mingwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本报告系统分析了图加密方案（GES）中单对最短路径（SPSP）查询的结构性泄漏问题，发现PathGES方案存在严重的重轻分解（HLD）不平衡（超过99%的token-路径映射为一一对应），并据此提出Fragment Tree攻击实现最高10.24%的精确恢复；同时提出增强方案BlindGES，通过Merge-and-Divide机制和两级多映射索引将一一对应映射降至20%以下，并将设置时间减少50%、存储开销降低32%、路径长度泄漏控制在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图加密方案旨在实现图数据的安全外包和高效查询，但现有方案在SPSP查询中存在未被充分认知的结构性泄漏问题。PathGES虽声称能抵抗查询恢复攻击，但其重轻分解存在严重不平衡，导致token-路径映射高度一一对应，从而暴露查询内容和路径长度等敏感信息。因此需要系统性地分析这些漏洞并提出更安全的防御方案。

Method: 本文采用三部分方法：(1)对PathGES进行深入的结构性分析，揭示HLD分解不平衡和token-路径映射过度一一对应的问题，并利用Falzon-Paterson攻击和侧信道推断路径长度；(2)提出Fragment Tree攻击，利用上述结构弱点从图加密索引中恢复查询内容；(3)设计BlindGES增强方案，引入Merge-and-Divide机制和两级多映射索引来消除一一对应映射，并附有安全证明和七个真实数据集上的实验评估。

Result: 实验表明：PathGES在真实数据集上超过99%的token-路径映射为一一对应，可被Falzon-Paterson攻击和侧信道攻击利用；Fragment Tree攻击在稀疏图上实现最高10.24%的精确查询恢复。BlindGES将一一对应映射降至20%以下，设置时间减少50%，存储开销减少32%，路径长度泄漏控制在1%以下。

Conclusion: 本文系统地揭示了GES中SPSP查询的结构性泄漏问题，证明了现有方案PathGES的安全性不足，并通过攻击与防御的对比研究验证了BlindGES在安全性和效率上的显著提升，为图加密方案的后续设计提供了重要的方向性参考。

Abstract: Graph encryption schemes (GES) enable secure outsourcing of graph data while supporting efficient queries. This report provides a comprehensive analysis of structural leakage in GES for single-pair shortest path (SPSP) queries, integrating findings from two recent works. First, we analyze PathGES, a scheme designed to resist query recovery attacks through heavy-light decomposition (HLD) and canonical fragment encoding. Our analysis reveals that PathGES suffers from significant imbalances in HLD decomposition, with over 99% of token-path mappings being one-to-one on real-world datasets, enabling both the Falzon-Paterson attack and side-channel inference of path lengths. Second, we present Fragment Tree attack that exploits these structural weaknesses to recover query contents, achieving up to 10.24% exact recovery on sparse graphs. Third, we introduce BlindGES, an enhanced scheme incorporating a Merge-and-Divide mechanism and two-level multimap index that reduces one-to-one mappings to below 20%, cuts setup time by 50%, reduces storage overhead by 32%, and limits path length leakage to under 1%. This report systematically presents attack methodologies, defense mechanisms, security proofs, and experimental evaluations on seven real-world datasets.

</details>


### [195] [P2Skill: Privacy Preserving Skill Distillation for Cloud-Local LLM Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14094)
*Myunghoon Ryu,Geunpyo Park,Sungjoon Lee,XinYu Piao,Jong-Kook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: P2Skill是一种基于提示的技能蒸馏方法，通过本地小语言模型(SLM)遵循云端大语言模型(LLM)生成的技能提示，自主完成分解、PII感知路由、改写和重建，在保护敏感用户数据的同时利用云端大模型的推理能力，无需隐私专用微调或训练辅助检测器，在四域基准上比以往基线高出1.69倍和3.66倍的隐私保护推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端大模型推理能力强但存在用户数据泄露风险，现有隐私保护方法（如提示扰动、实体掩码、模型微调）要么扭曲上下文语义，要么需要额外训练，缺乏一种不损害语义且无需额外训练就能保护PII的云端推理方案。

Method: P2Skill采用提示技能蒸馏框架：云端LLM通过执行失败的迭代反馈生成并精炼技能提示，本地SLM遵循这些技能提示执行四个步骤——分解(Decomposition)、PII感知路由(PII-aware Routing)、改写(Paraphrasing)和重建(Reconstruction)，从而在去除PII的同时保留查询语义，无需隐私专用微调或学习型辅助检测器。

Result: 在四个领域的基准评估中，P2Skill相比先前基线分别提升了1.69倍和3.66倍的隐私保护推理质量，表明其能有效保护PII并保持推理能力。

Conclusion: P2Skill证明了基于提示的技能蒸馏可以替代传统隐私保护训练方案，使本地SLM在不进行隐私专用微调的情况下泛化超越记忆的PII模式，实现云端大模型推理与本地数据隐私保护的有效平衡。

Abstract: Cloud-local LLM inference systems have the potential to use the reasoning capability of large cloud models while protecting sensitive user data on personal devices. Cloud-bound requests must exclude personally identifiable information (PII) to prevent external data leakage. Existing privacy-preserving methods rely on prompt perturbation, entity masking, or model fine-tuning, but these approaches may distort contextual semantics or require additional training. This paper proposes P2Skill, a prompt-based skill distillation method in which a local small language model (SLM) autonomously performs decomposition, PII-aware routing, paraphrasing, and reconstruction by following the skill prompts. Skills are iteratively refined from execution failures by a cloud LLM, enabling the local SLM to generalize beyond memorized PII patterns, and therefore P2Skill requires no privacy-specific fine-tuning or learned auxiliary detectors. Evaluation on a four-domain benchmark shows that P2Skill achieves $1.69\times$ and $3.66\times$ higher privacy-preserved inference quality than previous baselines.

</details>


### [196] [BGA: A noise-immune neural distillation framework for malicious signature extraction in high-entropy encrypted flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14126)
*Sheng Hong,Yixuan Huang,Weiwei Jiang,Junyuan Zhang,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijian Jiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BGA框架，一种针对高熵TLS 1.3流量的噪声免疫神经蒸馏框架，用于加密威胁情报。通过ANOVA解耦高判别性控制平面特征，结合WGAN-GP解决类别不平衡，并集成BiLSTM与自适应门控多头注意力机制来抑制加密噪声、增强恶意特征。在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT基准上各关键指标超过95.2%，推理延迟仅0.2820ms，具有工业边缘网关实时部署潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵TLS 1.3流量中存在注意力稀释问题，加密噪声干扰恶意威胁的检测，同时工业控制场景存在极端类别不平衡，需要对加密流量中的恶意威胁进行高效、鲁棒的检测。

Method: 1) 使用ANOVA方差分析从随机加密噪声中解耦高判别性的控制平面特征（如工业设定点）；2) 集成WGAN-GP模块（强制1-Lipschitz约束）合成高保真少数类样本以解决极端类别不平衡；3) 构建BiLSTM用于时序依赖提取，并结合自适应门控多头注意力机制作为神经滤波器，动态抑制加密伪影同时放大恶意签名。

Result: 在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT基准上所有关键指标超过95.2%；对罕见恶意状态命令注入（MSCI）攻击的检测召回率提升43.2%；噪声注入压力测试中比原始Transformer性能高出8.57%的边际；推理延迟低至0.2820ms（ARM理论扩展估计1.6920ms）。

Conclusion: BGA框架在加密工业流量威胁检测中展现了卓越的准确性、鲁棒性和实时性，为异构工业边缘网关上的实时部署和未来硬件实现提供了有前景的架构基线。

Abstract: To mitigate attention dilution in high-entropy TLS 1.3 flows, we propose BGA, a noise-immune neural distillation framework for encrypted threat intelligence.The methodology first employs Analysis of Variance (ANOVA) to decouple high-discriminatory control-plane features - specifically industrial setpoints - from stochastic cryptographic noise. To resolve the extreme class imbalance within a corpus of 86,878 flow records, a Wasserstein GAN with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) module, enforcing the 1-Lipschitz constraint, is integrated to synthesize high-fidelity minority samples, elevating the detection recall of rare Malicious State Command Injections(MSCI) attacks by 43.2%. At its core, the BGA architecture integrates Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) for temporal dependency extraction and an Adaptive Gated Multi-Head Attention mechanism. This gated unit functions as a neural filter to dynamically suppress encryption artifacts while amplifying malicious signatures. Extensive evaluations on CIC-IDS-2018 and Edge-IIoT benchmarks demonstrate a performance ceiling exceeding 95.2% across all key metrics. Furthermore, noise-injection stress tests confirm BGAs superior structural resilience with a 8.57% performance margin over vanilla Transformers, while its ultra-low inference latency of 0.2820 ms (estimated 1.6920 ms via theoretical scaling for ARM) indicates a high potential for real-time feasibility on heterogeneous industrial edge gateways, providing a promising architectural baseline for future hardware implementation.

</details>


### [197] [MazeRunner: Nonlinear Task and Clue Orchestration for LLM-driven Black-Box Automated Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14216)
*Zhenyuan Li,Yi Jiang,Junjie Cheng,Yaokun Li,Jing Qiu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MazeRunner是一个基于三智能体任务与线索编排框架的自主渗透测试系统，通过分离全局编排、上下文密集型执行和面向失败的审查，解决了真实世界黑盒渗透测试中非线性攻击图、模糊失败和长时程线索关联等挑战。在10个HTB目标上以47.7%的子任务完成率超越基线（PentestGPT-V2为36.2%，Claude Code为34.2%），并在6个目标上获得用户级及以上访问权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的渗透测试智能体主要在简化线性场景中执行端到端工作流，而真实黑盒测试本质上是非线性的：攻击图初始未知需逐步推断，观测可能揭示多个攻击分支，失败常模糊不清，关键线索跨度长。现有智能体容易陷入深度优先探索陷阱、误诊失败并遗忘先前证据。

Method: 提出MazeRunner，采用三智能体任务与线索编排框架：(1) 全局编排智能体负责宏观决策与分支切换；(2) 上下文密集型执行智能体负责具体攻击操作；(3) 面向失败的审查智能体负责错误诊断与动作修正。系统持续维护任务状态和环境证据，支持动作修订、前置条件恢复、分支切换和长距离线索关联。

Result: 在10个近期发布的HTB目标上，限制每次运行为2000万LLM token并无目标特定解决方案泄漏。使用Claude Sonnet 4.5时，MazeRunner完成47.7%的标注子任务（PentestGPT-V2为36.2%，Claude Code为34.2%）；在6个目标上获得用户级或以上访问权限，其中2个获得root权限；同模型基线仅在2个目标上达到用户级访问且从未获得root权限。执行轨迹分析显示MazeRunner探索更多攻击分支且更高效地获取shell。

Conclusion: MazeRunner通过任务与线索编排框架有效解决了LLM智能体在真实黑盒渗透测试中的核心局限，显著提升了子任务完成率、目标访问级别和攻击分支探索效率，为自动化渗透测试提供了更贴近实际场景的解决方案。

Abstract: Penetration testing is essential yet resource-intensive. Although large language models (LLMs) show promise for automating security auditing, existing agents mainly execute end-to-end workflows in simplified linear scenarios. Real-world black-box testing is fundamentally nonlinear: the attack graph is initially unknown and must be incrementally inferred from environmental feedback. Observations may reveal multiple attack branches, failures are often ambiguous, and critical clues may span long action horizons. Existing agents therefore tend to become trapped in depth-first exploration, misdiagnose failures, and forget prior evidence. We present MazeRunner, an autonomous penetration testing system built on a three-agent task-and-clue orchestration framework. It separates global orchestration, context-intensive execution, and failure-oriented review while persistently maintaining task states and environmental evidence. This design supports action revision, prerequisite recovery, branch switching, and long-range clue correlation. We evaluate MazeRunner on 10 recently released HTB targets, limiting each system-target run to 20 million LLM tokens and preventing target-specific solution leakage. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, MazeRunner completes 47.7% of annotated subtasks, compared with 36.2% for PentestGPT-V2 and 34.2% for Claude Code. It achieves user-level or higher access on six targets, including root access on two; each same-model baseline reaches user-level access on only two targets and never obtains root access. Execution-trace analysis further shows that MazeRunner explores more attack branches and acquires shells more efficiently.

</details>


### [198] [A Four-Axis Trustworthiness Benchmark for LLM-as-Judge in Principle-Based Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14329)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Principle-Bench基准，涵盖基于原则的金融监管（如英国FCA法规）中LLM-as-judge评估的四个关键维度：准确性、改写鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和校准性。同时提出Ceca（Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment），一种校准且可审计的评估器。实验发现没有任何方法在四个维度上全面占优，最强的120B LLM在对抗性关键词堆砌场景下准确率骤降47个百分点，且不同模型家族的分歧极大（Cohen's kappa=0.16），表明基于原则的监管需要报告对抗欺骗和事后校准指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于原则的监管（如"公平、清晰、不误导"或"提供良好结果"）中的评价标准无法简化为二元谓词，因此越来越多地使用LLM-as-judge作为替代。然而，这些LLM评判器缺乏系统性的多维度评估框架，特别是在对抗性鲁棒性和校准性方面存在盲区。

Method: 构建Principle-Bench基准：包含168个加密资产金融推广场景，映射到英国FCA两项原则，预注册规则生成改写、对抗性关键词堆砌和边界扰动变体。提出Ceca（Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment）评估器，能输出逐样本反事实归因且保证校准性。比较关键词计数、三种sentence-transformer嵌入器、开权重LLM评判器及校准级联方法。

Result: 没有任何方法在四个评估维度上全面占优。120B LLM评判器在良性输入上表现最强，但在关键词堆砌的Consumer Duty输入上准确率从0.74跌至0.27（损失47个百分点），构成"合规剧场"现象。不同模型家族的第二个评判器在同一分割上仅Cohen's kappa=0.16，证明了失败源于模型本身而非语料。

Conclusion: 任何用于基于原则监管的生产级LLM评判器，必须在报告总体准确率的同时，披露逐原则的对抗欺骗率（per-principle adversarial deception）和事后校准（post-hoc calibration）指标。

Abstract: Principle-based regulation, with evaluative standards such as "fair, clear, and not misleading" or "deliver good outcomes", cannot be reduced to binary predicates, and LLM-as-judge is increasingly used as the substitute. Our position is that any such judge must be evaluated on four axes: accuracy, paraphrase robustness, adversarial robustness, and calibration. We release Principle-Bench, 168 cryptoasset financial-promotion scenarios mapped to two UK FCA principles, with paraphrase, adversarial keyword-stuffing, and boundary perturbations authored under a pre-registered rubric; the first benchmark covering all four axes for principle-based regulation. We also introduce Ceca (Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment): a calibrated, auditable assessor that emits exact per-exemplar counterfactual attributions. Across keyword counting, three sentence-transformer embedders, an open-weight LLM-judge, and a calibrated cascade, no method dominates all four axes. A 120B LLM-judge, strongest on benign inputs, loses 47 accuracy points (0.74 to 0.27) on keyword-stuffed Consumer Duty inputs: "compliance theatre." A second judge from a different model family agrees only at Cohen's kappa = 0.16 on that split, localising the failure to the model rather than the corpus. Any deployment-grade LLM-judge for principle-based regulation must report per-principle adversarial deception and post-hoc calibration alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [199] [A Hybrid LLM-Based Framework for Automated Security Annotation Generation in Business Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14370)
*Md Kamrul Islam,Tiphaine Henry,Mattia Salnitri,Julius Köpke,Sami Souihi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合框架，结合LLM语义提取、模式约束映射、规则归一化和确定性验证，自动将BPMN流程模型和自然语言安全需求文档转换为符合SecBPMN2规范的安全注释。在27个流程模型上的评估显示，该框架生成的结构有效性和模式完整性高，精确率显著优于人工分析师（0.58 vs 0.29），召回率相当，错误注释减少近50%，且生成速度远超人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全业务流程的建模与分析需要在流程模型中融入安全注释，尽管已有BPMN扩展（如SecBPMN2），但从自然语言规范中推导准确完整的安全注释仍是手工、依赖专家且容易出错的任务，缺乏自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出一个混合框架，输入为BPMN流程模型和安全需求文档，通过LLM语义提取、模式约束映射、规则归一化和确定性验证四个步骤，自动生成符合SecBPMN2规范的安全注释。在27个跨领域流程模型上进行综合评估。

Result: 框架能一致地生成结构有效且模式完整性高的SecBPMN2注释。相比人类分析师，精确率显著更高（0.58 vs 0.29），召回率相当（0.52 vs 0.50），错误或位置不当的注释减少近50%，且生成速度明显快于手工标注。

Conclusion: 混合LLM与规则自动化方法能够减少建模工作量，同时提高一致性和可靠性，为安全设计BPM提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: The modelling and analysis of secure business processes require the incorporation of security annotations into process models. Although BPMN extensions, including SecBPMN2, exist for this purpose, the derivation of accurate and complete security annotations from natural-language specifications remains a manual, expert-intensive, and error-prone task. This paper presents a hybrid framework that takes a BPMN process model and a security requirements document as input and automatically generates security annotations adhering to the SecBPMN2 specification. The approach combines Large Language Model (LLM)--based semantic extraction with schema-constrained mapping, rule-based normalization, and deterministic validation. The framework is evaluated comprehensively on a curated dataset of 27 process models from various domains. The results indicate that it consistently produces structurally valid SecBPMN2 annotations with high schema completeness. Compared to human security analysts, the system achieves substantially higher precision (0.58 vs. 0.29) while maintaining comparable recall (0.52 vs. 0.50) and reduces erroneous or misplaced annotations by nearly 50%. In addition, annotation generation is significantly faster than manual annotation. These findings demonstrate that hybrid LLM- and rule-based automation can reduce modeling effort while improving consistency and reliability, thereby providing a scalable foundation for security-by-design BPM.

</details>


### [200] [STINER: Automated Extraction of Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence from X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14418)
*Yasir Ech-Chammakhy,Oussama Azrara,Jaafar Chbili,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出STINER，一个用于从社交媒体流中提取战略网络威胁情报的专家标注语料库与细粒度实体分类体系，包含2,100条真实告警和8类战略实体。实验表明领域自适应编码器DarkBERT达到89.33%的严格F1分数，优于通用基线和微调大语言模型，并通过欧洲2025上半年威胁态势分析验证了其实际价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 战略CTI（如识别目标行业、归因勒索软件组织、评估数据损失规模）如今越来越多地首先出现在X（前Twitter）上，往往比官方报告早数天。然而社交媒体文本的随意性和高度不规则方言使传统NER模型难以处理，造成自动化防御系统的盲区。亟需一个针对该场景的实体识别方案。

Method: 构建包含2,100条专家标注真实告警的数据集，提出覆盖8种战略实体类型（如Threat Actor、Sector、Location）的细粒度分类体系；对9个模型在12种配置下进行基准测试，涵盖通用与领域自适应编码器、开放schema抽取以及零样本/微调生成式LLM。

Result: 领域自适应编码器DarkBERT达到89.33%的严格F1分数，优于通用基线和微调大语言模型（后者还带来显著更高的推理延迟）。基于STINER-DarkBERT的欧洲威胁态势分析（2025上半年）与官方报告在主要目标上一致，同时揭示了西班牙攻击的独特可见性特征，并能提前捕捉到SafePay勒索软件活动的早期信号。

Conclusion: STINER证明了领域自适应NER模型是面向社交媒体战略CTI提取的高效且经济的方案，优于成本高昂的LLM微调方法；社交媒体驱动的实体抽取能在官方威胁态势报告回溯性描述之前，提供早期预警信号，为自动化防御提供增量价值。

Abstract: Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) focuses on high-level insights, such as identifying targeted industries, attributing attacks to specific ransomware groups, and assessing the scale of data loss. Today, X (formerly Twitter) has become the fastest source for this intelligence, often hosting real-time breach announcements days before formal vendor reports. Converting this raw chatter into actionable intelligence requires navigating a complex linguistic landscape. Conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) models struggle to parse the informal and highly irregular dialect of social media, creating a blind spot for automated defense systems. To address this challenge, we introduce STINER, a taxonomy and expert-annotated corpus for extracting strategic intelligence from social media streams. We construct a high-quality, expert-annotated dataset of 2,100 real-world alerts and propose a granular taxonomy of eight entity types centered on strategic pivots such as Threat Actor, Sector, and Location. We benchmark nine models across 12 evaluated configurations, spanning general-purpose and domain-adapted encoders, open-schema extraction, and generative LLMs in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings. Domain-adapted encoders such as DarkBERT reach a strict F1-score of 89.33%, outperforming both general-purpose baselines and fine-tuned Large Language Models, which additionally incur substantially higher inference latency. Leveraging STINER-DarkBERT, we conduct a European threat landscape analysis for H1 2025. Our results align with official reporting on major targets while highlighting the distinct visibility profile of attacks in Spain, and illustrate how social-media-driven extraction can surface early signals of the SafePay ransomware campaign prior to its retrospective characterization in vendor threat landscape reports.

</details>


### [201] [Lower Bounds on Black-Box Constructions of Pseudorandom Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14501)
*Bar Alon,Itai Dinur,Muthuramakrishnan Venkitasubramaniam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了从伪随机生成器（PRG）构建伪随机函数（PRF）的全黑盒构造的下界问题。针对一个受限但通用的构造类（其归约与敌手交互次数独立于敌手内部查询次数），证明了任何此类构造至少需要 Ω(n/log n) 和 Ω(in/log in) 次非自适应 PRG 调用，即使在弱 PRF（单比特输出、查询为 i.i.d. 均匀随机）情形下也成立。此外，对输出足够长的弱 PRF，即使构造允许自适应查询，也证明了相应的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自 Goldreich, Goldwasser 和 Micali (1984) 首次构造 PRF 以来，该构造需要调用 PRG ω(log n) 次。尽管 Beimel, Malkin 和 Mazor (2024) 证明了在树构造族中 GGM 构造最优，但能否仅用一次 PRG 调用构建 PRF 这一基本问题仍然悬而未决。本文旨在从全黑盒构造的角度，为 PRF 从 PRG 构建所需的最少调用次数提供更强的下界。

Method: 本文考虑全黑盒构造（构造与归约均为黑盒），并限定归约与敌手的交互次数与敌手在每次交互中对其底层函数的 oracle 调用次数无关。在此类构造模型中，作者证明了任何构造都必须使用 Ω(n/log n) 和 Ω(in/log in) 次非自适应 PRG 调用，其中 n 为 PRG 输入长度，in 为 PRF 输入长度。该不可能性结果即使在弱 PRF（单比特输出、敌手仅做独立同分布均匀随机查询）场景下也成立。进一步，对输出足够长的弱 PRF，作者还给出了允许自适应 PRG 查询时仍然成立的下界。

Result: 本文的主要结果是证明了在上述受限全黑盒模型下，不存在使用 o(n/log n) 或 o(in/log in) 次非自适应 PRG 调用的 PRF 构造。该不可能性对单比特输出的弱 PRF（敌手仅做 i.i.d. 均匀随机查询）同样成立。此外，对于输出足够长的弱 PRF，即使允许构造进行自适应 PRG 查询，下界依然成立。

Conclusion: 本文为 PRF 从 PRG 的全黑盒构造提供了新的非平凡下界，表明单次或少量 PRG 调用不足以构建 PRF（在本文考虑的构造类中）。这进一步缩小了 GGM 构造最优性的适用范围，为未来研究更一般的构造模型或寻找更紧的界指明了方向。

Abstract: In their seminal work, Goldreich, Goldwasser, and Micali [CRYPTO 1984] constructed a pseudorandom function (PRF) using a black-box access to a pseudorandom generator (PRG). When combined with Levin's domain extension technique, the GGM construction invokes the PRG $ω(\log n)$ times, where $n$ denotes the input length to the PRG. To this day, no black-box construction achieving fewer calls is known.
  Recently, Beimel, Malkin, and Mazor [CRYPTO 2024] showed that for a certain family of constructions, which they termed \emph{tree constructions}, the GGM construction is optimal. However, the basic challenge of whether a PRF can be built with just \emph{one invocation} of the PRG still remains open.
  In this work, we consider fully black-box constructions of PRFs from PRGs, where both the construction and the reduction are required to be black-box, and the number of interactions the reduction makes with the adversary is independent of the number of oracle calls the adversary makes to its underlying function within each interaction. Our main result shows that no such construction can have $o(n/\log n)$ and $o(\mathsf{in}/\log\mathsf{in})$ \emph{non-adaptive} calls to the PRG, where $\mathsf{in}$ is the input length of the PRF. This impossibility holds even for weak PRFs with one-bit output, where the adversary is restricted to making i.i.d. uniformly random queries. In addition, we prove a lower bound for weak PRFs with sufficiently long outputs that holds even when the construction is allowed to make adaptive queries to the PRG.

</details>


### [202] [Trust Without Boundaries: An Architectural Analysis of Satellite Flight Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14532)
*Jack Vanlyssel,Gruia-Catalin Roman,Kendra Cook,Sazzadur Rahaman,Afsah Anwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析NASA核心飞行软件（cFS）的内部信任边界问题。通过NOS3模拟器，实验证明单个被攻破的机载组件可利用广泛共享的权限实施难以与正常行为区分开的攻击，并对比其他模块化飞行软件框架，指出信任假设缺陷，提出未来飞行软件内部信任边界加固机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器日益软件化和互联化，机载飞行软件成为关键安全边界。然而主流飞行软件架构将机载组件视为可信任的对等体，简化了集成却限制了内部隔离和访问控制，存在严重安全隐忧。

Method: 从权威性、身份、通信、可观测性和持久性五个维度系统分析NASA cFS架构；利用NASA飞行代表性NOS3模拟器，通过五个实验植入恶意机载组件来利用合法架构特权验证弱点；与其它模块化飞行软件框架对比，识别普遍存在的信任假设和架构弱点。

Result: 实验证明，单一被攻破的组件可以利用广泛共享的权威实施攻击，且攻击行为在可观测性上难以与合法行为区分；模块化飞行软件框架之间存在共性信任假设缺陷。

Conclusion: 提出架构层面的启示，并讨论在未来飞行软件系统中加强内部信任边界的具体机制，以提升整体安全性。

Abstract: As spacecraft become more software-driven and interconnected, onboard flight software is an increasingly important security boundary. Popular flight software architectures often treat onboard components as trusted peers, simplifying integration while limiting internal isolation and access control. We analyze NASA's Core Flight Software (cFS) to examine how authority, identity, communication, observability, and persistence are distributed across onboard components. Using NASA's flight-representative NOS3 simulator, we validate these weaknesses through five experiments implemented with a malicious onboard component that abuses legitimate architectural privileges. We then compare cFS with other modular flight software frameworks to identify recurring trust assumptions and architectural weaknesses. Our results show that a single compromised component can exploit broadly shared authority in ways that are difficult to distinguish from legitimate behavior. We conclude with architectural implications and discuss mechanisms for strengthening internal trust boundaries in future flight software systems.

</details>


### [203] [Finding Vulnerabilities via LLM-Augmented Semantics-Aware Type-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14533)
*Ruizhe Wang,Meng Xu,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SETYPE，一种基于语义的类型系统，利用大语言模型从源代码中提取符号和表达式的自然语言含义进行类型推断与检查，以检测软件漏洞。原型PYSETYPE在真实Python Web应用中达到87%精度和88%准确率，并发现15个潜在零日漏洞，其中9个得到开发者确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态分析漏洞检测依赖安全专家将不安全编码模式编码为语法规则，但这种方法主要关注语法模式，忽略了代码中更深层的语义信息（如变量和函数名的含义）。随着软件系统日益复杂，仅依靠语法规则建模漏洞变得越来越困难。

Method: 提出SETYPE语义感知类型系统，可直接从源代码中基于符号和表达式的自然语言含义推导而来。在该类型系统中，类型推断和类型检查均由大语言模型（LLMs）执行，类型检查失败即表明存在潜在漏洞。基于此实现了PYSETYPE原型，用于检测Python Web应用中的漏洞。

Result: 在真实世界应用上的评估中，PYSETYPE实现了87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率。使用PYSETYPE识别出15个潜在零日漏洞，其中9个已被开发者确认。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，基于LLM的语义感知类型系统SETYPE在漏洞检测方面是可行且有效的，能够超越传统语法规则的局限性，发现真实世界中的零日漏洞。

Abstract: Vulnerability detection via static analysis traditionally relies on security experts encoding insecure coding patterns into algorithmic rules. However, this approach often focuses on syntactic patterns and overlooks deeper semantic information in the code, such as the meanings of variable and function names. As software systems grow more complex, modeling vulnerabilities using only syntactic rules becomes increasingly challenging.
  In this paper, we propose a semantics-aware approach to detecting software vulnerabilities. We present SETYPE, a semantics-aware type system that can be derived directly from source code based solely on the meanings of symbols and expressions in natural language. In the SETYPE type system, both type inference and checking are performed by Large Language Models (LLMs), and a failed type check indicates a potential vulnerability.
  We prototype PYSETYPE to demonstrate the feasibility of SETYPE for detecting vulnerabilities in Python web applications. Our evaluation on real-world applications achieves 87% detection precision and 88% detection accuracy. Using PYSETYPE, we identified 15 potential zero-day vulnerabilities, nine of which were confirmed by developers.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RubricForge通过从少量带真实标签的轨迹中反射式演化出评判性rubric（文本评分标准），用一个冻结的7B模型同时作为agent和评判者，在不访问环境奖励的情况下进行奖励无关评估。虽然与通用G-Eval风格评判者相比，原始一致性没有显著提升（McNemar p=0.248），且绝对分数校准略逊，但它在最关键的部署指标——假阳性率上表现更好（tau-bench上0.115 vs 0.173），即更少将失败轨迹错误地评为成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模评估语言模型agent时，常用另一个语言模型作为自动评判者（自动裁判），因为真实的环境奖励信号昂贵、缓慢或部署时不可用。然而现有评判方法（如G-Eval手写评分标准，或微调评判者权重）往往倾向于把流畅但不成功的轨迹误判为成功，缺乏可信度。作者希望将评判者的评分标准植根于真实的标注结果，以提高可靠性。

Method: RubricForge的方法包括：(1) 从一小部分带ground-truth标签的轨迹中，通过反射式演化（reflective evolution）迭代生成评判rubric文本；(2) 以最大化与真实环境奖励的一致性为目标优化该rubric；(3) 冻结最终rubric，在一次模型调用中对held-out轨迹进行评分，全程无需环境访问。输出的rubric为人类可读文本，每个判定都可追溯到命名标准。

Result: 使用一个冻结的7B模型同时作为agent和评判者，在tau-bench（220次rollout中取173条标注轨迹）和WebShop（160条）上测试。主要收益是忠实度（faithfulness）而非原始一致性：与G-Eval相比，一致性差异无统计学显著性（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略差（|err|差-0.048，p=2x10^-4）。但RubricForge的假阳性率约为G-Eval的一半（tau-bench上0.115 vs 0.173），三次过度信用被捕获且零反转；WebShop排序更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对于奖励无关的评估器，假阳性率才是部署相关的重要指标——假阳性会"放行"一个有缺陷的agent，而假阴性仅浪费一次重试。RubricForge虽然在整体一致性上未显著超越通用评判者，但显著降低了假阳性率，提供了更可追溯、更可信的评估依据，更适合作为部署时的奖励替代信号。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [205] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）进行了系统的逐层敏感性分析，利用基于幅度的专家掩蔽在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上进行评估。核心发现是层敏感性具有强深度依赖性：早期和中层高度脆弱，而晚期层（尤其35-39层）能容忍激进的低幅值专家掩蔽。晚期聚焦策略在掩蔽640-1,145个专家时仍保留249-255/300的Good+Similar输出，远超均匀全层掩蔽的150/300。此外，将top-k路由宽度从8降至6可显著减少墙钟时间且无质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构已被广泛采用以在保持计算效率的同时扩展LLM规模，但各MoE层在压缩中的相对重要性尚未得到充分刻画。作者旨在通过系统的逐层敏感性分析填补这一空白，为深度感知的MoE专家掩蔽提供经验基础。

Method: 在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上，对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层、256专家/层、top-8路由）进行基于幅度的专家掩蔽实验。采用多阶段研究，涵盖100、300和500个提示词的评估规模，跨三台H100 GPU服务器。对比了平坦全层掩蔽与晚期聚焦掩蔽策略，并额外探索了top-k路由宽度从8降至6的影响。

Result: 研究发现层敏感性强烈依赖于深度：早期层（0-9）和中间层（10-29）对专家掩蔽高度脆弱，而晚期层（30-39）尤其很晚期层（35-39）能容忍激进的低幅值专家掩蔽。30%平坦全层掩蔽仅保留150/300的好+相似输出，而晚期聚焦策略在掩蔽640-1,145个专家的同时保留249-255/300。在500提示的保留验证集上，很晚期窄策略（层35-39 @ 50%）获得最佳质量/掩蔽专家权衡，保留419/500同时仅掩蔽10,240个总专家中的640个。将top-k从8降至6在100提示探针上显示出显著的墙钟时间减少且无质量损失，但尚未与激进专家掩蔽干净地组合。

Conclusion: 研究为深度感知的MoE专家掩蔽提供了实证基础，确立了向物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分和基于训练恢复推进的实际路径。晚期层的韧性表明存在结构化的冗余模式，可以利用该模式实现更高效的MoE模型压缩。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [206] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过电路分析发现，大语言模型（LLMs）在46个任务中发展出与人类大脑相似的模块化架构：同一认知域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的任务在LLM中招募重叠的神经元，而不同认知域的任务则招募不同的神经元。这种模块性在生物大脑和人工神经网络中的趋同涌现，提示其可能是智能系统的根本属性，而非进化上的偶然事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑表现出显著的功能特化，不同神经网络分别支持语言、形式推理、心智推理和物理推理等高阶认知功能。作者试图探究这种模块化组织究竟是智能系统构建的根本性原则，还是生物大脑在进化过程中的偶然产物。通过检验经过完全不同优化过程产生的另一类智能系统——大语言模型——是否也涌现出类似的组织结构，可以为这一问题提供关键证据。

Method: 作者使用电路分析（circuit analyses）方法，对覆盖四个认知域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的N=46个任务进行了系统分析。通过考察不同任务在大语言模型内部所招募的神经元群体及其重叠程度，比较LLM中的模块化模式与人类大脑的功能网络组织之间的对应关系。

Result: 研究发现LLM确实发展出与人类大脑相镜像的模块化架构：在人类中调用同一网络的任务，在LLM中会招募重叠的神经元；而在人类中调用不同网络的任务，在LLM中则招募不同的神经元。这种模块化组织的趋同涌现跨越了生物大脑和神经网络两类截然不同的智能系统。

Conclusion: 模块性在大脑和神经网络的趋同涌现表明，模块化组织可能并非生物进化的偶然产物，而是智能系统的一种根本性属性。这一发现对理解智能的组织原则以及设计更具可解释性的人工智能系统具有重要启示。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [207] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Chutes平台为期一年的生产环境中大规模语言模型（LLM）服务请求追踪数据，从全局特征和时间纵向研究两个维度分析了真实世界的LLM服务工作负载。与既有研究不同，该追踪数据覆盖多个模型和用户的完整生产行为（包括热门和长尾模型），并从聚合、时间、模型级和用户级四个视角揭示负载演化规律和用户-模型交互结构，且完整的一年级追踪数据将与论文一同发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务工作负载研究在规模和范围上受限，通常观测时间周期短，对生产中用户与模型交互方式的可见性有限，无法充分捕捉LLM服务工作负载随时间演化的规律以及用户-模型交互对生产流量的塑造作用。

Method: 采用Chutes平台为期一年的生产追踪数据，从全局特征与纵向研究两个角度，结合聚合、时间、模型级和用户级四个分析视角，对LLM服务工作负载进行系统分析，揭示隐藏于聚合视图之下的负载演化和用户-模型结构。

Result: 揭示了通常被聚合视图掩藏的工作负载演化规律和用户-模型交互结构，覆盖了包括热门和长尾模型在内的多个模型和用户的完整生产行为。同时，将发布完整的一年追踪数据以供后续研究使用。

Conclusion: 通过对真实生产环境中一年期的LLM服务追踪数据进行多维度分析，加深了对真实世界LLM服务工作负载的理解，并公开完整数据以支持下游研究，无需依赖采样或合成生成的工作负载。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [208] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentao，一个受治理的本地优先运行时系统，用于工具调用型LLM代理。通过分层架构将模型生成的动作提议与主机授权的执行分离，将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹作为显式运行时抽象，使代理更可治理、可检查且适合主机控制的本地环境。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理作为执行系统调用工具、修改本地状态、使用持久化记忆并与外部协议交互，带来了权限过度、审计性弱、提示注入、工具投毒和不可控副作用等风险。现有系统缺乏对这些风险的显式运行时级治理机制。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构，包括主机面对的表面层（host-facing surfaces）、主机契约（host contract）、运行时核心、权限中介的工具系统，以及支持记忆、回放、插件、技能、子代理和协议集成的子系统。核心思想是将模型生成的动作提议（action proposals）与主机授权的执行（host-authorized execution）分离，通过结构化事件接口暴露执行过程。

Result: Agentao展示了如何将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹构建为显式运行时抽象，使代理系统更具可治理性和可检查性。文章说明了其动机、威胁模型、设计目标、治理模型、执行管线和结构化事件接口，但明确指出该系统不提供正式的安全保证。

Conclusion: Agentao提供了一种以治理为核心的本地优先代理运行时参考设计，强调显式化和可审计性而非形式化安全保障，为构建更可控的LLM代理执行系统提供了实践基础。代码已公开供社区使用和扩展。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [209] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI评估范式应从"超人类自主表现"转向"人机协作团队表现"，认为当前以替代人类为目标的评估导向是错误方向，应转向评估人机协同以产生更好的社会效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的AI评估范式聚焦于超人类自主性能，隐含的目标是替代人类，这正在将AI开发引向错误方向。作者认为这种导向不利于社会整体利益。

Method: 作为立场论文（position paper），通过论辩方式提出应转向评估人机团队（human-AI teams）的性能，强调AI应作为人类能力的真正补充物而非替代品。

Result: 论文提出了一种评估范式的转向建议：从单方面评估AI自主能力，转变为评估"人类-AI"协作团队的整体表现。

Conclusion: 作者认为这种协作式转向将培育出真正补充人类能力的AI系统，从而带来远比现行评估过程更好的社会结果。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [210] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中高置信度错误的一种新解释——"稳定误校准"（stable miscalibration），即模型在错误答案上保持高置信且局部稳定，而非简单的脆弱推理。作者提出两种诊断方法：输出层面的审计评分（audit score）和内部敏感性探针（hidden-state sensitivity probe），并在多领域二元事实审计集与三个开源模型上进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的高置信度错误常被视为内部推理脆弱性的证据，但本文提出另一种可能性：即部分高置信度错误是"稳定"的，意味着模型在局部扰动下仍坚持错误答案，这种稳定误校准时无法用脆弱的推理失败来解释，需要用新的诊断框架加以区分。

Method: 采用双重诊断框架：(1) 输出层面：标签感知的审计评分，基于强制作答基线，按置信度变化与过度自信错误对领域进行排序；(2) 内部层面：敏感性探针，测量提示扰动下隐藏状态的运动幅度。在包含多领域二元事实的审计集上，结合abstention-aware的自我批评（self-critique）范式，对三个开放权重模型逐层分析内部敏感性变化。

Result: 审计评分能够追踪自我批评减少决策损失的效果，但直接使用带标签基线排序该增益更强；自我批评提示一致地降低了跨层的隐藏状态敏感性，表明这种效应是提示诱导的局部稳定化；然而，审计定义的过度自信错误在敏感性上并未显著高于自信正确回答，说明部分高置信错误确实是稳定且误校准的。

Conclusion: 高置信度错误不应一律归因于推理脆弱性；存在一类稳定且误校准的高置信错误，需将输出层面审计与内部敏感性分析结合才能更准确地区分，且自我批评的稳定化效果不等同于校准改善。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [211] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为一致性度量（BCM），通过训练模型从代理执行的轨迹特征预测任务成功，并为每条轨迹生成特征归因向量，再计算向量间的平均成对相似度来量化代理系统在任务间的一致性。基于约9000条轨迹的实验表明，跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是两个可分离的维度，且一致性无法归结为成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理评估几乎完全依赖成功率的结局指标，无法捕捉代理在过程中行为的一致性。作者认为行为一致性是独立且可度量的性质，需要在结局指标之外补充过程层面的可靠性信号。

Method: BCM方法：1）训练一个模型，从代理执行轨迹的行为特征预测任务成功；2）为每条轨迹生成特征归因向量；3）计算一个代理系统内这些向量之间的平均成对相似度。使用约9000条来自六个语言模型代理在软件工程任务上的轨迹进行实证。

Result: 核心发现：跨任务与任务内一致性是两个可以发散的独立维度——某些系统在单任务重复尝试上表现一致（局部可复现），却缺乏跨任务的稳定策略（全局碎片化）；一致性不能归结为成功率，成功率相近的系统在一致性上可能差异显著；在控制任务难度的条件下，前沿模型与开源模型之间的一致性差距仍然存在。

Conclusion: BCM可作为过程层面的可靠性信号，补充现有结局指标；作者同时明确说明了该指标有意义的适用条件。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [212] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了在AI中介通信时代中误解检测问题，将误解视为一个分层过程（分歧产生→放大→检测修复或持续存在），综合九个领域文献，识别出十一种精确故障模式，将其映射到八个分析层，并从信息与通信理论角度形式化建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信已从实时面对面交互转向AI中介渠道，这使得人们失去了传统对话修复所依赖的资源，而新的检测手段构建速度跟不上变化。误解检测成为亟待解决的问题，但现有研究分散在不同领域且缺乏系统性分类。

Method: 整合九个通常互不引用的研究领域的观点，识别十一种故障模式并按其作用位置（产生分歧、放大分歧、控制检测与修复）分类；从文献中推导出八个分析层而非套用现有模型；形式化扩展信息与通信理论，从信号传输扩展到意义重构；提供逐源证据矩阵、编码手册及九个对话案例分析。

Result: 识别出十一种故障模式，其中八种主要产生分歧、两种放大已有分歧、一种决定分歧是否被检测并修复；建立了八层分析模型并进行了形式化建模；提供了可审计的证据矩阵和编码手册等辅助材料。

Conclusion: 本文提出的误解分类体系是首个同时做到"将机制定位于过程中特定点"和"按功能对机制分类"的分类框架，为AI中介通信环境下的误解检测与修复提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [213] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种主动感知框架用于具身目标消歧，以主动观察为核心获取信息，利用视觉语言模型在继续观察、请求澄清或完成目标选择之间决策，并通过真实机器人实验验证该框架将物理信息获取与用户意图澄清统一于具身消歧流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言为机器人提供了灵活的任务接口，但具身环境中的目标歧义不仅来自用户意图，还可能源于当前观察中缺乏与任务相关的物理证据。现有交互式消歧方法主要依赖向用户提问获取额外信息，而遮挡、受限视点、不可读文本和未观测目标等场景需要机器人主动改变观察状态，因此需要一种以主动观察为主的信息获取机制。

Method: 提出一个主动感知框架：以主动观察作为信息获取的主干，使用视觉语言模型基于累积的视觉证据和交互信息进行决策，判断是继续观察、请求用户澄清，还是完成目标选择。主动观察既能直接恢复缺失的判别性证据，也能揭示物体名称、标签和语义属性，从而在仍需用户澄清时提升澄清质量。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，该框架能够将物理信息获取与用户意图澄清统一在一个具身消歧过程中，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 以主动观察为核心、由视觉语言模型驱动的感知决策框架能够有效解决具身目标消歧问题，实现了物理证据采集与用户意图澄清的有机结合。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary. Real-robot experiments show that the framework combines physical information acquisition and userintent clarification within a unified embodied disambiguation process.

</details>


### [214] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MobileMem，一个面向设备端长期记忆研究的基准与框架，基于年度规模的移动体验数据，通过知识驱动的合成管道构建连贯且时序一致的长时间轨迹，支持文本与多模态两种设置，覆盖多跳推理、时序推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断，推动记忆从信息检索迈向体验智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代AI代理正从回答孤立问题转向持续性个人助理，需要长期记忆来积累和利用用户特定经验。然而现有基准在现实移动场景下不足，因为移动端的经验具有异构性、多模态性、演化性和深度个性化特征，缺乏一个能够真实反映这些特点的评测基准。

Method: MobileMem包含两个核心组件：(1) 一个知识驱动的合成管道，用于从用户-应用会话中构建连贯且时序一致的长时间轨迹；(2) 互补的文本与多模态评测设置，覆盖多跳推理、时序推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断四个任务维度，使代理能够记忆过去、理解当下并适应未来。

Result: 论文提出MobileMem作为一个可用的基准与框架，基于年度规模移动体验数据构建，能够有效评测代理在长时记忆上的表现，涵盖多跳与时序推理、知识更新及偏好推断等核心能力。

Conclusion: MobileMem通过建模体验而非孤立事实，将记忆从单纯的信息检索扩展到连续个人学习中的体验智能，为设备端长期记忆研究提供了系统性评测基础。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [215] [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607)
*Jia Sheng,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何在前沿大语言模型更新过程中预测样本级别的"退化"（regression），即旧模型回答正确而新模型回答错误的情况。作者在三个任务族（多选题、数学推理、代码生成）和六组模型更新对上，系统比较了单模型信号（置信度、logit边际、注意力熵）与跨版本信号（输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表征漂移）的预测能力。研究发现信号有效性具有任务依赖性、没有普适最优信号，并提出了一个概念验证的选择性回退机制，可将高风险样本路由回旧模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型频繁更新，虽然新模型在总体性能上通常优于旧版本，但聚合指标掩盖了样本层面的退化现象——某些样本在新模型下反而从正确变为错误。现有研究缺乏对这些"回归"信号的系统性评估，从业者难以预测哪些样本会在模型更新后退化。

Method: 采用统一"增量价值测试"（added-value test）框架，以置信度作为基线，逐一评估每种信号在其基础上带来的额外预测增益。信号分为两大类：单模型信号（模型自身输出的置信度、logit边际、注意力熵）和跨版本信号（新旧模型输出之间的KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表征漂移）。实验覆盖六个基准、三个任务族和六组模型更新对。

Result: （1）信号有效性依赖于任务类型：置信度在多项选择和更简单的数学问题上最强，而似然/KL类信号在更难的数学和代码生成任务上更频繁地带来增益；（2）没有任何信号在所有模型更新对上都表现最佳；（3）部分跨版本信号即使在置信度失效时仍保持信息量，且无需标签即可发挥作用。

Conclusion: 研究为从业者提供了任务层面的信号选择指南，使其能针对特定模型更新选择最可靠的回归预测信号。同时，所提出的选择性回退概念验证为"高风险样本路由回旧模型"的实践提供了支持，代码已开源。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are updated frequently and typically outperform their predecessors in aggregate. But aggregate gains say little about individual samples: an update can still cause sample-level regression, where a response correct under the old model becomes incorrect under the new one. This paper studies how to predict such regressions from signals available at inference time. We compare single-model signals (confidence, logit margin, attention entropy) against cross-version signals (output KL divergence, likelihood drift, token-level KL, representation drift) under a unified added-value test that isolates each signal's gain over a confidence baseline. Across six benchmarks in three task families (multiple-choice question answering, or MCQ; math reasoning; code generation) and six model update pairs, we find that (1) signal effectiveness is task-dependent: confidence is strongest on MCQ and simpler math, while likelihood/KL signals give the most frequent gains on harder math and code; (2) no signal is universally best across model updates either; and (3) some cross-version signals stay informative even when confidence fails, including without labels, which supports a proof-of-concept selective fallback that routes high-risk samples back to the old model. Practitioners can use these task-level patterns to choose which regression signal to trust for a given update. Code is available at https://github.com/jiashengsally/llm-regression-signals.

</details>


### [216] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需标注基准即可评估持续学习ARAI（LLM+检索/记忆）系统效果的方法，通过强教师模型对弱学生模型进行稀疏纠正，以学生向教师收敛的程度作为代理指标，与金标准相关性得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全场景中，传统基于标注基准的评估方法因标签稀缺、陈旧且不具代表性而失效，LLM-as-a-judge也无法提供有效信号，从业者难以判断学习ARAI（持续学习harness）是否有效或孰优孰劣。

Method: 提出基于缩放假设的端到端评估框架：强教师模型向带持续学习harness的弱学生模型提供稀疏纠正，通过衡量学生随时间向教师收敛的程度来评分的harness；并在多个安全任务、模型家族和harness设计上验证教师相对提升与金标准提升的相关性。
