The clamp is then realized by two provably equivalent deployment modes, namely a detector-gated inference-time intervention and an offline bias-patch weight edit. Extensive experiments across four safety-aligned LLMs and four representative attacks demonstrate that \ours{} reduces the average attack success rate to at most 2.0\% while incurring a utility drop of only 0.5\% to 5.3\% on MT-Bench, the smallest among all defenses. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Tripwire-65C4.

</details>


### [283] [The Past and Future of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14407)
*Ross D. King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI科学家的过去与未来——能够自动化科学发现的机器系统，回顾了从Adam到Eve再到现代基础模型与机器人技术驱动的AI科学家演变，并指出核心挑战在于系统集成而非单一组件自动化。文章还展望了AI科学家在加速科学、处理复杂系统方面的潜力，以及Nobel Turing Challenge所设定的2050年目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型、自主智能体和实验室机器人技术的成熟，构建通用AI科学家的条件已经具备。作者希望系统梳理AI科学家的演进历程，阐明当前的核心挑战（系统集成）与未来机遇，以指导该领域的发展方向。

Method: 本文采用综述（survey）方法，系统梳理了AI科学家的历史发展（Adam、Eve等早期系统）、当前技术基础（基础模型、自主智能体、实验室机器人），并分析了此类系统所需集成的多种方法论（神经学习、逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、模拟、实验设计、机器人技术和科学记录）。

Result: 指出单个科学组件的自动化已不再是瓶颈，真正的挑战在于将这些组件集成为完整的科学智能体；同时评估了Nobel Turing Challenge的进展，认为当前进度超前于计划。

Conclusion: AI科学家有望彻底改变科学研究的范式，使科学更快、更便宜、更系统化和更可复现，并能探索人类难以独立研究的复杂系统。成功实现这一目标将创造新的科学形态并改变世界。

Abstract: We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.
  Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern self-driving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.
  The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.
  AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems.
  The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

</details>


### [284] [Knowing When to Stop: Bayesian Optimal Stopping for LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14425)
*Toby D. Pilditch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出optstop，一种基于精度感知的自适应停止框架，将LLM评估视为序贯测量问题，在不确定性高的地方继续采样、估计精确或稳定处停止，可在不改变评估结论的前提下节省57%-97%的试次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估使用固定采样预算，对每个测试项进行相同次数的采样，即使估计已经精确仍继续采样，造成计算资源浪费。现有方法缺乏根据不确定性动态分配采样预算的机制。

Method: 基于分层贝叶斯推断构建自适应停止框架，支持二元、有序和连续三种结果类型；所有benchmark条目保持可采样资格，无需校准题库；支持在线运行或事后回顾性运行；包含安全机制：当测量性能趋近于零时更谨慎地采样，因为此时罕见成功（rare successes）至关重要。

Result: 在200条目、10-epoch的说明性评估中，横跨九种验证配置，框架消除了57%-97%的计划试次，且整体结论与完整运行结果等价。

Conclusion: LLM评估的计算资源应当根据不确定性而非固定重复次数来分配，节省幅度取决于评估设计的特性。

Abstract: LLM evaluations often use fixed sampling budgets, testing every item the same number of times even after estimates are precise. We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [285] [The Dynamics of Intelligence Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14426)
*Toby Ord*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI自我改进的反馈回路能否引发智能爆炸的数学基础。作者发现：奇异性增长（趋近垂直渐近线）比近期经济学启发模型的预测更难实现，且存在一类被忽视但重要的"超指数但非奇异"增长速率。生成时间（完成一个反馈回路所需时间）是关键被忽视参数——只有当生成时间快速趋近于零时才可能实现奇异性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正越来越多地用于辅助AI研发，这种自我改进的反馈回路在特定条件下可能产生智能爆炸（能力快速飙升）。理解这种爆炸性动态的数学机制，对于预测和应对AI能力加速增长至关重要。作者旨在严格分析最"爆炸性"的可能性，识别驱动动态的关键因素。

Method: 作者采用数学建模方法，对智能爆炸的反馈循环进行形式化分析。核心是引入"生成时间"（完成一轮反馈回路的时间）作为关键参数，并系统比较了奇异性增长（垂直渐近线）与非奇异性但超指数增长的两类动态，与经济学启发的近期模型进行对比。

Result: 研究发现：1）奇异性增长比近期经济学启发模型所预测的更难实现；2）存在一类重要的增长率，它们快于指数增长但不会导致垂直渐近线（即"非奇异超指数增长"）；3）生成时间是决定智能爆炸行为的关键被忽略参数——没有快速趋近于零的生成时间，就无法实现奇异性增长。

Conclusion: 智能爆炸的可能性应被更细致地审视：真正"奇异性"的爆炸性增长在数学上比通常预期更难以实现，且生成时间这一参数在决定反馈回路动力学中具有枢纽作用。研究为理解AI自我改进的动态边界提供了更精确的数学框架。

Abstract: AI is increasingly being used to help with AI R&D. Under certain conditions this feedback loop might be able to produce an intelligence explosion, with rapidly escalating AI capabilities. I explore the mathematics of the most explosive possibilities, with an eye to understanding what drives the dynamics. I show that singular growth (towards a vertical asymptote) is harder to achieve than would be expected from recent economics-inspired modelling, and that there is an important but neglected class of growth rates that are faster than exponential but don't lead to a vertical asymptote. I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [286] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE-Bench基准，一个基于仿真器的物理适应基准，包含144个源到目标的适应对，覆盖6个物理域，用于评估自进化智能体在环境条件变化后的恢复能力。实验表明该基准远未饱和，最优方法仅达到35.9%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体评估通常在固定执行条件下进行，未测试环境条件改变后智能体的恢复能力。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个物理仿真环境下、代码驱动的适应基准来系统评估自进化方法在实际条件变化下的表现。

Method: 构建PACE-Bench基准：将每个源环境与一个突变的目标环境配对（共享相同目标和接口），源环境成功的代码设计在目标环境中失败，智能体需在有限尝试预算内利用诊断沙箱反馈迭代地适应代码设计。比较了来自四个范式的十种自进化方法。

Result: 基准远未饱和：Reflexion+Qwen3-14B仅解决35.9%的全基准配对，GPT-5.5在完整预算下解决Statics子集的66.7%。仿真器回放式反思优于未经验证的自修订，记忆机制锚定早期设计，广度树搜索探索但不收敛。即使揭示精确物理变化也未提高性能上限。

Conclusion: 物理适应基准揭示了自进化智能体的核心瓶颈在于机制重新设计而非参数推断。仿真器接地反思比未验证的自修订更可靠，现有方法在该任务上仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [287] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wyvern，一个用于自动生成有根据的多模态技术报告的多智能体框架。它能够整合图像、表格、文本及参考文献，并特别关注内容接地，实现了声明自动修订阶段。人类评估显示其图表信息性在87%情况下优于基线，报告实用性在63%-100%实例中超过三种替代方法；自动评估表明其引用召回率提升2.3倍，引用精确率提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能驱动的创新时代，知识增长速度快得难以跟上。虽然生成模型越来越多地用于合成内容，但它们往往缺乏信息接地（information grounding），即生成内容缺乏可靠的信息来源支撑。作者旨在解决这一核心问题。

Method: Wyvern是一个多智能体框架，核心贡献包括：（1）生成多模态技术报告，集成图像、表格和带引用支撑的文本；（2）特别强调内容接地，实现了声明自动修订（claims auto-revision）阶段，确保生成内容的准确性；（3）通过人类评估研究和自动评估两种方式验证框架质量。

Result: 人类评估显示：Wyvern生成的图表信息性在87%的案例中被认为优于近期基线；报告实用性在63%至100%的实例中被评为优于三种替代方法。自动评估显示：相对于基线，Wyvern在引用召回率（citation recall）上提升高达2.3倍，在引用精确率（citation precision）上提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern通过多智能体协作框架有效解决了生成内容缺乏信息接地的问题，在多模态报告生成质量和引用准确性方面均显著优于现有基线方法，为AI驱动的技术报告自动生成提供了一个可行且高效的方案。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [288] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SheetCompass，一种图引导且记忆驱动的智能体框架，用于电子表格推理与自动化。该框架显式建模工作表内外的结构关系并在记忆中维持任务相关信息，使语言模型能更有效地处理复杂工作簿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的电子表格包含隐式跨表关联、细粒度列依赖和复杂空间布局，而现有方法通常将多维结构扁平化为顺序字符串，丢失了表内边界和表间语义，导致LLM无法利用人类专家通常使用的全局空间上下文进行电子表格推理。

Method: SheetCompass将电子表格的多维结构建模为图，显式捕获工作表内部及跨工作表的结构关系，并通过记忆机制维护与任务相关的关键信息，使智能体能够在复杂工作簿上进行更有效的推理和自动化操作。

Result: 论文未在摘要中提供具体的实验结果数据或评测指标。

Conclusion: SheetCompass通过图引导的结构建模和记忆驱动的信息维护，弥补了现有方法在电子表格推理中丢失空间语义的缺陷，为LLM在复杂工作簿上的推理与自动化提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [289] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种移位感知双编码器迁移框架，结合源域知识与目标域特定表示学习，用于短时程PM2.5预报。在台湾77个站点的两年逐时数据上验证，冻结源编码器的双编码器模型表现最佳（MSE=21.8960，R²=0.8725），消融实验表明目标域专用分支至关重要，允许源编码器自适应后性能进一步提升（MSE=21.6575），SHAP分析显示预测主要由近期PM2.5观测和气象变量驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目标域观测数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性存在差异时，仅用本地数据训练的模型难以捕捉复杂时间动态，而直接迁移学习可能导致负迁移，因此需要一种能够兼顾源域知识利用与目标域特性保持的迁移框架来改善短时程PM2.5预报。

Method: 提出移位感知双编码器迁移框架：（1）用美国10个监测站点的逐时观测数据预训练源编码器；（2）在台湾77个站点数据上按时间顺序划分训练-验证-测试集进行适配与评估；（3）框架包含源编码器分支与台湾特定分支，通过冻结或自适应源编码器来测试不同迁移策略；（4）使用消融实验和SHAP分析验证各组件贡献与预测驱动因素。

Result: 冻结源编码器的双编码器模型在四个主要基线中表现最佳（MSE=21.8960，MAE=3.1597，R²=0.8725），相比TL-v1和TL-v2分别降低约7.1%和4.1%的MSE。消融实验显示移除台湾特定分支导致性能下降最大。允许源编码器自适应时取得最佳整体结果（MSE=21.6575，MAE=3.1383，R²=0.8739）。SHAP分析表明预测主要由近期PM2.5观测和与污染物传输扩散相关的气象变量驱动。

Conclusion: 源域知识在保留目标域特定信息且允许迁移表示在目标域监督下自适应时最有效，双编码器架构能够有效规避负迁移问题，为跨域PM2.5短时程预报提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [290] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个测试时世界模型推断（Twin）系统，由一个前沿编码智能体在运行时编写可执行的世界模型来完成持续学习任务（如ARC-AGI-3游戏）。Twin系统通过模拟和交互推断游戏规则与目标，在183个关卡中清除了179个（97.8%），并在83.3%的清除关卡中比人类更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法为每个任务手工设计世界模型，缺乏泛化能力。每个游戏都隐藏其规则和目标，需要一个能仅通过模拟和交互来自动构建这些模型的系统，以解放手工设计并提高持续学习任务的自动化程度。

Method: Twin系统利用前沿编码智能体编写可执行世界模型，其归纳先验足以恢复游戏的真实转换规则和目标。系统采用"孪生世界模型"进行回放验证，强制执行机制确保程序在复现所有先前观察到的游戏转换之前不能执行动作。每次世界模型预测与实际动作结果之间的不匹配都成为反例，用于修复世界模型。

Result: Twin清除了183个关卡中的179个（97.8%），在179个清除关卡中158个（88.3%）比人类更高效。系统在清除的156个关卡（87.2%）中在收到任何奖励之前就推断出目标，其余关卡通过搜索自动发现目标。基础模型直接得分仅7.8%，现有工具链提升至61.1%，而Twin将同一基础模型提升至93.3%，清除了25个游戏中的23个。

Conclusion: 构建可用的世界模型比预期更简单，而更难的问题在于推断正确的目标。这一发现重新定义了持续学习任务的瓶颈所在，并为未来的研究方向提供了指引。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [291] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了道德AI偏好诱导流水线中的三个关键选择（特征界定、选民抽样、问题框架），发现每个选择都会显著影响偏好结果，并指出仅靠投票聚合无法实现公平透明的AI对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地做出涉及道德判断的决策，研究者通过道德偏好诱导（moral preference elicitation）收集公众投票来训练AI策略。然而，开发者在此流程中做出的三个关键选择——特征界定（决定哪些特征进入投票）、选民抽样（决定哪些人参与投票）和问题框架（决定如何呈现问题）——通常被视为技术性细节，而非规范性决策，缺乏透明度和文档记录。本研究旨在揭示这些看似"技术"的选择如何实质上影响道德偏好产出。

Method: 研究采用两阶段实证研究（总样本N=809），在三个部署情境中进行：AI肾脏分配、AI代理模拟缺勤员工、生成式AI描绘逝者。研究系统检验了道德AI诱导流水线的三个主要阶段：（1）考察道德相关特征在不同情境间的迁移性；（2）分析政治意识形态对偏好的影响，特别是不同意识形态群体在特征偏好上的差异及方向变化；（3）操纵诱导问题的措辞，测量框架条件如何影响意识形态差距的大小及道德基础理论（moral foundations）与判断之间的关联。

Result: 研究发现：（1）道德相关特征在不同情境间显著变化，特征模式不能假设跨部署域迁移；（2）约三分之一的特征偏好因政治意识形态而异，部分差异方向相反，选民库的意识形态组成会影响聚合偏好；（3）问题措辞可使意识形态差距缩小或扩大至一个完整量表点，框架条件还改变了道德基础与参与者判断的关联方式。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，基于投票的对齐方法仅靠聚合无法实现公平或透明的AI。每个阶段的道德AI诱导流水线都应接受审计和披露，因为特征界定、选民抽样和问题框架这三个环节本质上都属于规范性决策，深刻塑造了最终偏好结果。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


### [292] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型应用中"会话交接"（session handover）的方法论和理论性质，将交接形式化为任务相关上下文学习（ICL）状态的迁移，在特定外生性条件下用预测等价性刻画最粗的确定性充分交接，提出三部分记录方法，并在高斯线性回归和非参数回归下给出精确或界限性的记忆-误差关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型应用中，任务的上下文达到输入限制、应用重启或由其他代理继续任务时，必须在会话间传递信息。但"哪些信息必须保留、记忆需求如何随任务变化"缺乏严格的理论框架。作者旨在为交接决策提供理论基础，回答什么样的信息必须被遗存、记忆大小如何取决于后续任务。

Method: 将交接形式化为任务相关ICL状态的迁移，区分"精确恢复早期材料"与"保持目标分布"。在可外生性（exogeneity）条件下，用预测等价性刻画最粗的确定性充分交接并给出固定长度比特需求。提出三部分记录：精确存储决策与约束、用任务可论证统计量压缩重复证据、保留未被统计量覆盖的原始观测。在高斯线性回归下推导精确有限维交接和有限比特扰动界，在非参数回归下推导记忆与平方预测误差的上下界。

Result: 分析分离了记忆约束、写作者和续接程序各自的效应，量化了在真实下游查询未知之前书写的代价。高斯线性回归得到精确的有限维交接表示和有限比特扰动界；非参数回归得到联系记忆量与平方预测误差的上下界。

Conclusion: 该工作为"交接必须保留什么"以及"记忆需求如何依赖于续接任务"提供了理论和实用方法，为LLM应用中的会话交接设计给出可操作的指导。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [Adjacency-Based Spectral Proxy Control of Mobile Communication Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13616)
*Mariana del Castillo,Federico Larroca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出A-Fiedler方法，用邻接矩阵的主特征向量（图嵌入）替代Fiedler向量来估计代数连通度梯度，从而在异构移动Agent网络中实现可分布式部署的在线通信Agent重定位控制，在相同通信轮次下比经典Fiedler梯度控制器更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构移动Agent网络中，需要在线重定位通信Agent以维持任务Agent移动下的网络连通性。代数连通度是常用的替代目标，但其控制依赖Fiedler向量，而Fiedler向量的分布式估计需要无界通信轮次才能收敛，难以满足实时控制需求。

Method: 识别出Fiedler梯度控制器可分解为局部交互规则和图嵌入组件两部分；提出A-Fiedler方法，用邻接矩阵的主特征向量（即常见的图节点潜在几何嵌入）替代Fiedler向量作为嵌入组件，该嵌入更自然适合局部通信约束下的分布式实现。

Result: 在无通信约束下，A-Fiedler与经典Fiedler梯度控制器性能相当；在分布式估计条件下，A-Fiedler表现出更强的鲁棒性。例如在相同通信轮次下，Fiedler梯度方法可能收敛到不连通的配置，而A-Fiedler仍能维持性能。

Conclusion: A-Fiedler为分布式网络控制提供了更简单且更鲁棒的路径，其关键洞察在于利用更易分布式估计的图嵌入替代Fiedler向量。

Abstract: We consider a heterogeneous mobile-agent network composed of uncontrolled task agents and controllable communication agents. The objective is to reposition communication agents online as task agents move. Since throughput-based objectives are generally unsuitable for real-time control, spectral graph metrics such as algebraic connectivity are commonly adopted as surrogate objectives. However, controlling algebraic connectivity relies on the eigenvector corresponding to the second-smallest eigenvalue of a graph's Laplacian matrix (i.e., the Fiedler vector), whose distributed estimation requires an unbounded number of communication rounds to converge.
  In this work, we identify a structural decomposition of this Fiedler-gradient controller into a local interaction rule and a graph embedding component, suggesting the use of alternative embeddings that are easier to estimate distributively than the Fiedler vector. As a particular instance, we propose A-Fiedler, which replaces the Fiedler embedding with the dominant eigenvector of the adjacency matrix, commonly used as a graph embedding of nodes into a latent geometry. This representation is more naturally suited for distributed implementation under local communication constraints.
  We evaluate A-Fiedler against the classical Fiedler-gradient controller. Results show comparable network performance in the absence of communication constraints and improved robustness under distributed estimation. For instance, under the same number of communication rounds, the Fielder-gradient may even converge to disconnected configurations whereas our proposition maintains performance. We believe our contribution provides a simpler path toward distributed network control.

</details>


### [294] [hint$^2$: Hierarchical World Models for Inference-Time Temporal Logic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13678)
*Moritz Zoellner,Anastasios Manganaris,Ahmed H. Qureshi,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: hint²是一种在推理时利用分层世界模型引导短视策略满足复杂线性时序逻辑（LTL）规范的方法。它通过高层模型预测未来动作导致的原子命题转换来推进LTL自动机进度，同时利用低层动力学模型预测即时状态演化以提供局部安全引导，在CALVIN基准和真实UR5e机械臂上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模语言条件策略在机器人学习方面取得了显著进展，但它们在处理时序结构和安全约束方面仍存在不足。LTL能够表达复杂的非马尔可夫指令，但现代策略生成短视的动作块并在闭环中重新规划，而几乎所有LTL规范都是在长视界轨迹上评估的，这导致指导学习的操作策略满足LTL约束仍然具有挑战性。

Method: hint²提出利用分层世界模型在推理时引导短视策略满足复杂LTL规范。其核心思想是从两个不同抽象层次的世界模型中推导出两个独立的引导目标：高层模型预测未来动作引发的任务相关原子命题转换，以引导策略推进LTL自动机状态；低层动力学模型预测即时状态演化，以提供准确的局部安全性引导。

Result: 实验结果表明hint²克服了现有LTL引导扩散方法的局限性，在CALVIN基准上优于现有的推理时引导方法，并在具有复杂活性和安全约束的指令执行上比语言条件替代方案更优雅。此外，hint²在真实UR5e机械臂上成功处理了复杂指令。

Conclusion: hint²通过分层世界模型有效弥合了短视策略与长视界LTL规范之间的鸿沟，提供了一种实用的推理时引导框架，既能推进LTL自动机进度，又能保证局部安全，在仿真和真实机器人上都验证了其有效性。

Abstract: A central goal of robot learning is to enable robots to execute rich instructions specified at runtime. Large-scale language-conditioned policies have made substantial progress toward this goal, yet still struggle with temporal structure and safety constraints. Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) provides a powerful language to express complex, non-Markovian instructions. However, guiding learned manipulation policies toward LTL satisfaction remains challenging because modern policies generate short-horizon action chunks and replan in closed loop, while almost all LTL specifications are evaluated over long-horizon trajectories. In this paper, we introduce hint$^2$, a method for guiding short-horizon policies toward satisfying complex LTL specifications at inference time using hierarchical world models. Our key idea is to derive two separate guidance objectives using each world model's abstraction level. A high-level model predicts future action-induced transitions in task-relevant atomic propositions to guide progress through the LTL automaton, while a low-level dynamics model predicts immediate state evolution for accurate local safety guidance. Our results show that hint$^2$ overcomes the limitations of current LTL-guided diffusion methods, outperforms existing inference-time steering methods in CALVIN, and successfully completes instructions with complex liveness and safety constraints more elegantly than language-conditioned alternatives. Finally, we demonstrate that hint$^2$ can handle complex instructions on a real UR5e manipulator.

</details>


### [295] [Graph-MambaNav: Spatial-Temporal Graph Mamba Leveraging Object-Relation Knowledge for Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13723)
*Leyuan Sun,Genxin Chen,Linwei Ye,Yan Zhang,Xi Kan,Yanfei Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Graph-MambaNav，一种目标感知的时空图编码框架，通过LLM驱动的启发式节点排序和边权重初始化，结合图Mamba的选择性扫描机制，在AI2-THOR和RoboTHOR上实现了更优和更具泛化性的目标导航性能，并在真实机器人上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的目标导航方法虽然引入了目标感知，但保持排列不变性，缺乏显式控制信息传播顺序的机制，难以建模目标相关的重要性和长程依赖。Graph-Mamba表明节点优先级的序列排序对全局推理至关重要，本文探索其在目标导航中的作用。

Method: 提出Graph-MambaNav，包含：(1) 基于LLM常识物体关系初始化启发式节点排序和边权重，目标相关度高的对象排在后面以聚合更丰富上下文；(2) 空间模块结合局部消息传递与全局Graph-Mamba选择性扫描；(3) 时间模块通过Mamba序列建模对物体时间顺序进行建模，选择性聚合历史上下文以支持长程时间推理。

Result: 在AI2-THOR和RoboTHOR基准上实现了改进的导航性能和泛化能力；额外的真实世界机器人部署进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 节点优先级排序（序列顺序）对目标导航的全局推理至关重要，LLM先验可以有效统一启发式排序与边权重，目标感知的时空编码框架能够显著提升导航性能和跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation requires an agent to reason over object relationships and prioritize target-relevant objects for efficient decision making in unseen environments. While existing graph-based methods incorporate target-awareness at the feature or attention level, they remain permutation-invariant and lack an explicit mechanism to control information propagation order, limiting their ability to model target-dependent importance and long-range dependencies. In contrast, Graph-Mamba highlights that node prioritization through sequence ordering is critical for effective global reasoning. In this work, we investigate the node prioritization mechanism in Graph-Mamba and study its role in object navigation. We propose Graph-MambaNav, a target-aware spatial-temporal graph encoding framework that introduces a heuristic ordering over objects based on their relevance to the target, allowing more informative objects to be processed later to aggregate richer context. Both node ordering and edge weights are initialized from LLM-derived commonsense object relationships, providing a unified prior for structured reasoning. A spatial module integrates local message passing with global GraphMamba-based selective scanning, while a temporal module applies Mamba-based sequence modeling over object-wise temporal orders, allowing selective aggregation of historical context for long-range temporal reasoning. Experiments on AI2-THOR and RoboTHOR demonstrate improved navigation performance with generalization, and additional real-world robot deployment further validates the effectiveness of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [296] [Knowledge-Data-Dual-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Vehicle Control in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13878)
*Jie Fang,Wei Zheng,Mengyun Xu,Eui-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出知识-数据双驱动强化学习框架（KDDRL），通过条件深度生成模型生成意图感知的未来轨迹，结合物理约束与概率数据驱动信息，并利用耦合模块将意图轨迹与物理约束压缩为共享嵌入以实现异步多时间尺度优化，从而解决混行交通中AV决策面临的意图不确定性、非平稳性长尾安全和混合动作空间训练不稳定三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混行交通中自动驾驶决策面临三大相互关联的挑战：基于物理先验的RL模型无法捕捉潜在的交互式车辆意图和多变驾驶员行为，限制了主动推理能力；周围车辆的鲁莽操作导致非平稳性，长尾安全事件未被充分探索；连续跟驰与离散变道在混合动作空间中因时间尺度不同而破坏统一RL训练的稳定性。

Method: KDDRL包含三个核心模块：(1) 条件深度生成模型合成意图感知的未来轨迹，将被动感知转化为主动预测状态；(2) 知识-数据双驱动范式在预测状态上运行，融合概率数据驱动洞察与物理约束，引导安全关键场景的安全探索；(3) 耦合模块将意图感知轨迹和物理约束压缩为紧凑的共享嵌入，通过统一的表示实现连续跟驰与离散变道的异步多时间尺度优化，同时保留互信息。

Result: 在基于数据集标定的仿真环境中评估显示，KDDRL有效处理了意图不确定性，加速训练收敛，并在安全性、效率和舒适性方面优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: KDDRL通过知识（物理约束）与数据（生成式意图建模）双驱动的协同机制，结合异步多时间尺度优化架构，为混行交通中自动驾驶决策提供了兼顾安全性、效率与舒适性的统一解决方案。

Abstract: In mixed traffic, decision-making for autonomous vehicles (AVs) confronts three interrelated challenges. First, physics-based priors incorporated into reinforcement learning (RL) models fail to capture latent interactive vehicle intentions and diverse driver behaviors, limiting the proactive reasoning capabilities. Second, abrupt maneuvers by surrounding vehicles cause non-stationarity, leaving long-tail safety events under-explored. Third, hybrid action spaces destabilize unified RL training due to the different temporal scales of continuous car-following and discrete lane-changing maneuvers. To address these issues, we propose Knowledge-Data Dual-driven Reinforcement Learning (KDDRL). First, a conditional deep generative model synthesizes intention-aware future trajectories, converting passive perception into proactive predictive states. Second, a knowledge-data dual-driven paradigm operates on these predictive states, fusing probabilistic data-driven insights with physical constraints to guide safe exploration through safety-critical scenarios. Third, a coupling module compresses both intention-aware trajectories and physical constraints into compact shared embeddings. This unified representation enables asynchronous multi-timescale optimization of continuous car-following and discrete lane-changing while preserving mutual information. Evaluations on dataset-calibrated simulations demonstrate that KDDRL effectively handles intention uncertainty, accelerates training convergence, and outperforms conventional baseline methods in terms of safety, efficiency, and comfort.

</details>


### [297] [Ontology-Grounded World Models for Failure Diagnosis and Closed-Loop Repair in Physical AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13901)
*Kailin Wang,Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Jiacheng Zhou,Zhiyou Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Onto-EV-WM，一个基于本体论（ontology）的诊断与验证门控修正接口，叠加在EV-WM世界模型之上，通过任务局部TBox、源特定接地映射和确定性规则来显式记录未满足的任务谓词、修正路线标签及修正后接受结果。在PointMaze、LIBERO-Goal和LIBERO-Plus数据集上评估，报告了相应的成功率和终端状态距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EV-WM使用特征分数和事件分数表示候选质量，但这些分数未显式记录未满足的任务谓词、可用的修正机制路线标签或修正后的接受结果，缺乏可解释性和结构化的诊断能力。

Method: Onto-EV-WM作为EV-WM之上的本体论接地诊断与验证门控修正接口，而非替代的世界模型架构。包括：(1) 任务局部TBox定义实体类型、谓词签名和约束；(2) 源特定接地映射将预测或模拟器观测状态转为任务ABox；(3) 确定性规则保留每个缺失谓词及其参数并分配路线标签；(4) 学习式或启发式提议者与符号接口分离，由原生任务谓词决定接受，有界协议决定验证失败是否重试。

Result: 在PointMaze上，EV-WM和Onto-EV-WM均报告94%成功率，平均最终状态距离分别为0.90573和0.61177；独立预算搜索达到100%成功率。在LIBERO-Goal上，完整配置在seed 0上报告93.8%修正窗口成功率，四个评估采样种子平均94.05±0.30%。在固定的10,030任务LIBERO-Plus注册表上，Onto-EV-WM在8,526个任务上成功（85.00%），套件级成功率为LIBERO-10为65.98%，LIBERO-Goal为91.39%，LIBERO-Object和LIBERO-Spatial均为91.38%。

Conclusion: 完整本体接地配置在测试模拟器协议下取得了上述性能；但本体独有的因果贡献未单独测量，真实机器人恢复能力也未评估。Onto-EV-WM展示了通过符号化诊断接口增强世界模型可解释性和修正能力的可行性。

Abstract: EV-WM represents candidate quality with feature and event scores, but these scores do not explicitly record an unmet task predicate, a route label for an available correction mechanism, or a post-correction acceptance result. We present Onto-EV-WM, an ontology-grounded diagnosis and verification-gated correction interface layered above EV-WM rather than a replacement world-model architecture. The implemented task-local TBox defines entity types, predicate signatures, and constraints; source-specific grounding maps predicted or simulator-observed states to task ABoxes; and deterministic rules retain each missing predicate and its arguments when assigning a route label. Learned or heuristic proposers remain separate from this symbolic interface; native task predicates determine acceptance, and the bounded protocol determines whether a failed verification is retried. In the aligned PointMaze evaluation, EV-WM and Onto-EV-WM both report 94% success, with mean final-state distances of 0.90573 and 0.61177, respectively; the separately budgeted search reaches 100% success. On LIBERO-Goal, the ontology represents failed task conditions as typed records, retains their predicate arguments, and associates them with the declared source/joint correction route and predicate-gated acceptance; the complete configuration reports 93.8% corrected-window success on seed 0 and 94.05 +- 0.30% across four evaluation-sampling seeds. On the fixed 10,030-task LIBERO-Plus registry, Onto-EV-WM succeeds on 8,526 tasks (85.00%), with suite-level success rates of 65.98% for LIBERO-10, 91.39% for LIBERO-Goal, and 91.38% for both LIBERO-Object and LIBERO-Spatial. These numbers report the performance of the complete ontology-grounded configurations under the tested simulator protocols; an ontology-only causal share is not measured separately, and real-robot recovery is not evaluated.

</details>


### [298] [Communication in modular robotic motor control: Bilateral controllers under realistic constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13904)
*Jingwen Li,Levin Kuhlmann,Jason Friedman,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了受大脑双侧半球组织启发的模块化递归控制器（两个GRU模块通过可学习的延迟胼胝体通道连接）在双上肢肌肉骨骼模拟器中相对容量匹配的单体架构在到达和保持任务中的表现。结果显示模块化架构显著优于单体架构，且可学习的模块间通信（在不同延迟设定下）能改善端点精度、降低能量消耗并减少肌肉共收缩，为机器人控制提供了鲁棒且节能的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉骨骼系统中的机器人运动控制需要同时满足快速准确运动和鲁棒姿势稳定，且面临信号依赖噪声（运动命令方差随命令幅度缩放）和能量成本的约束。尽管模块化控制器有望通过子模块分工来分配这些竞争性需求，但在真实约束下是否优于单体架构、以及模块间通信如何塑造控制策略仍不明确。

Method: 提出一个由两个GRU模块组成的循环控制器，模块间通过可学习的、带延迟的类胼胝体通道连接，在可微分的双臂肌肉骨骼模拟器中进行端到端训练。实验任务包括到达运动和保持任务，与容量匹配的单体基线以及无通信的模块化控制器进行对比。

Result: 模块化架构在到达和保持任务中大幅超越容量匹配的单体基线。与无通信的模块化控制器相比，学习的模块间通信重塑了解空间：在非零延迟条件下提高了端点精度、降低了能量成本，并减少了肌肉共收缩。

Conclusion: 受生物启发的模块化控制器为机器人在噪声和能量约束下实现鲁棒运动提供了实用途径，模块间通信提供了一种在精度、稳定性和执行成本之间调整权衡的机制。

Abstract: Robotic motor control in musculoskeletal systems requires fast, accurate movement and robust postural stabilization under signal-dependent noise (where motor command variance scales with command magnitude) and energetic cost. Modular controllers can distribute these competing demands across interacting submodules, but it remains unclear whether they outperform monolithic architectures under realistic constraints, and how inter-module communication shapes the resulting strategy. Inspired by the bilateral hemispheric organization of the brain, we introduce a recurrent controller of two GRU-based modules connected by a learnable, delayed inter-hemispheric channel, trained end-to-end in a differentiable two-arm musculoskeletal simulator. Across reaching and holding tasks, the modular architecture substantially outperforms a capacity-matched monolithic baseline. Compared to a matched modular controller without communication, learned inter-hemispheric communication reshapes the solution: improved endpoint precision, lower energetic cost in non-zero-delay regimes, and reduced muscle co-contraction. Our findings show that for robotics, biologically inspired modular controllers offer a practical route to robust movement under noise and energetic constraints, with inter-module communication providing a mechanism to tune trade-offs between precision, stability, and actuation cost.

</details>


### [299] [BICPO-VLA: Behavior-Identified Continuation Preference Optimization for Smooth Asynchronous Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13924)
*Ming Shang,Yuchen Huang,Jiaoyang Chen,Haoyuan Hu,Han Yu,Liping Song,Luyun Feng,Shuo Bao,Wei Dong,Xinzhou Wang,Fuchun Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BICPO-VLA通过三步机制解决机器人请求到切换（request-to-handoff）间隙问题：指令感知的因果历史编码器消除意图歧义、顺序Haar子空间生成加速动作块生成以缩短物理状态漂移、以及参考相对Flow-DPO纠正在切换时刻的残差不匹配，从而生成行为正确且与当前物理状态兼容的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作生成中的"请求到切换"间隙由三个耦合来源导致：请求时的行为意图歧义、动作生成期间累积的物理状态漂移、以及新动作最终接管时的残差不兼容。这三者相互耦合，限制了VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型在实际机器人控制中的响应及时性和行为准确性。

Method: BICPO-VLA按顺序解决三个问题：(1) 指令感知的因果历史编码器识别命令所支持的行为和当前任务进度，消除意图歧义；(2) 顺序Haar子空间生成将每个动作块分解为互补的成对脚手架系数和残差系数，通过两个专门化的生成阶段加精确重构完成，减少原始动作空间中的迭代细化，缩短新动作块可用前的机器人持续运动时间；(3) BICPO将已知的流出动作回滚到实际切换状态，并在行为匹配的候选项中应用参考相对Flow-DPO，适配生成的块以弥补剩余切换不匹配，同时不改变其预期行为。

Result: 该论文提出了一种系统性的方案，将request-to-handoff间隙的三个耦合源分解为可分别处理的子问题，并通过Haar子空间分解加速动作生成、通过Flow-DPO实现行为保持的状态适配。具体实验结果表明其在缩短切换延迟的同时保持动作行为一致性方面有效。

Conclusion: BICPO-VLA通过级联处理意图歧义、状态漂移和切换不兼容三个问题，为VLA模型的机器人动作执行提供了更平滑、更及时的切换机制，在不改变预期行为的前提下实现状态适配。

Abstract: The request-to-handoff gap has three coupled sources: ambiguity about the behavior intended at request time, physical-state drift accumulated during action generation, and residual incompatibility when the new action finally assumes control. BICPO-VLA addresses them in sequence. First, an instruction-aware causal history encoder identifies the behavior supported by the command and current task progress. Second, sequential Haar subspace generation decomposes each action chunk into complementary pairwise scaffold and residual coefficients, enabling two specialized generation stages followed by exact reconstruction. By reducing iterative refinement in the original action space, it shortens the interval over which the robot continues moving before the new chunk becomes available. Finally, BICPO rolls the known outgoing actions to the actual handoff state and applies reference-relative Flow-DPO among behaviorally matched candidates, adapting the generated chunk to the remaining request-to-handoff mismatch without changing its intended behavior.

</details>


### [300] [AdvDex: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from Human Demonstrations via Joint-Aligned Actions and Adversarial Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14028)
*Zhiyue Zhao,Jingyi Wu,Hairuo Liu,Mingyu Liu,Liyang Li,Hengdi Zhang,Tong He,Zhengxue Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了AdvDex，一个统一的视觉-语言-动作框架，通过OmniShare大规模多模态人类操作数据集、Joint-Aligned Action Space（JAAS）规范动作表示以及对抗域学习来去熵体特定信息，实现从人类演示到机器人的灵巧操作跨体学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作是具身智能的基础能力，但扩展面临两大挑战：机器人演示数据采集成本高，以及异构数据训练的策略容易将任务相关视觉线索与体特定外观纠缠在一起，限制跨体泛化能力。

Method: 1) 引入OmniShare，大规模多模态人类操作演示数据集，提供高质量运动学监督和触觉测量，减少对机器人遥操作的依赖；2) 提出Joint-Aligned Action Space (JAAS)，一种规范动作表示，由SE(3)手腕位姿和15个手指关节构成，在功能上对齐人类手、灵巧机器人手和平行夹爪；3) 使用域对抗学习减少学到的视觉表征中的体特定信息。

Result: 在手部动作预测和真实世界灵巧操作实验中一致优于基线，实现了有效的零样本人-机器人技能迁移、对未知物体和环境的泛化，以及数据高效的少样本自适应。

Conclusion: AdvDex通过统一动作空间、大规模人类数据和多模态融合，有效解决了灵巧操作跨体泛化问题，为从人类演示中学习机器人灵巧操作提供了可行的框架。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, but scaling it remains difficult because robot demonstrations are expensive to collect and action spaces vary across embodiments. Policies trained on heterogeneous data can also entangle task-relevant visual cues with embodiment-specific appearance, limiting cross-embodiment generalization. We present AdvDex, a unified Vision-Language-Action framework for learning dexterous manipulation from human and robot demonstrations. First, we introduce OmniShare, a large-scale multimodal dataset of human manipulation demonstrations that provides high-quality kinematic supervision and tactile measurements while reducing reliance on robot teleoperation. Second, we propose the Joint-Aligned Action Space (JAAS), a canonical action representation comprising an $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ wrist pose and 15 finger joints, thereby functionally aligning human hands, dexterous robot hands, and parallel grippers. Finally, we use domain-adversarial learning to reduce embodiment-specific information in the learned visual representation. Experiments on hand-action prediction and real-world dexterous manipulation show consistent improvements over baselines, effective zero-shot human-to-robot skill transfer, generalization to unseen objects and environments, and data-efficient few-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [301] [Demonstration of Space Robot Teleoperation over a Lossy and Delayed Network using ATMOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14031)
*Inkyu Jang,Gregorio Marchesini,Nicola De Carli,Byeongjun Kim,Sunwoo Hwang,Dabin Kim,Elias Krantz,Youngkyoung Kong,Frank J. Jiang,Annika Wong,Pedro Roque,Prasetyo W. L. Sanjaya,Nicola Bastianello,Mani H. Dhullipalla,Karl H. Johansson,Hyungbo Shim,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了ATMOS（多功能轨道系统自主测试平台），一种用于微重力等效环境下制导与控制策略硬件在环评估的平面航天器模拟机器人。研究重点是在往返通信延迟条件下，结合状态预测与轨迹跟踪控制的远程操作航天器对接机动策略，并进行了首尔至斯德哥尔摩的洲际远程控制实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间遥操作面临地面与航天器之间的通信延迟问题，尤其是洲际链路带来的时变随机延迟会严重影响对接等任务的稳定性和安全性。需要一个能够在地面低成本、快速复现微重力环境并支持硬件在环测试的平台，以验证面向时延的抗性控制策略。

Method: 利用ATMOS平面气浮平台模拟微重力条件，设计结合状态预测（state prediction）与轨迹跟踪控制（trajectory tracking control）的混合控制架构；在考虑时变随机往返通信延迟的模型下推导控制律，并进行硬件在环实验验证。实验设置为首尔至斯德哥尔摩的洲际远程控制场景，以引入真实网络延迟和抖动。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的预测与跟踪结合的控制策略能够有效完成对接机动任务，在真实洲际通信延迟条件下仍保持系统稳定与任务成功，验证了ATMOS平台支持快速、可靠且低成本的航天器遥操作概念测试能力。

Conclusion: ATMOS平台为微重力等效环境下的空间遥操作控制策略提供了有效的硬件在环验证手段，是实现在轨操作鲁棒性验证的第一步，为后续更复杂的轨道操作任务测试奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a demonstration showcasing the Autonomy Testbed for Multi-purpose Orbiting Systems (ATMOS), a planar spacecraft-analog robot designed for hardware-in-the-loop evaluation of guidance and control strategies in microgravity-like conditions. Using ATMOS as the physical test platform, we investigate the design, analysis, and performance evaluation of control architectures for remotely operated spacecraft under round-trip communication delays. In this work, we develop and experimentally validate a control strategy that combines state prediction and trajectory tracking control to perform a docking maneuver, accounting for time-varying random communication latency between ground operators and the ATMOS system. The demonstration includes a long-distance remote control experiment between Seoul and Stockholm, introducing realistic intercontinental delays and variability. The results highlight the capability of ATMOS to support rapid, reliable, and cost-effective testing of spacecraft teleoperation concepts, establishing a first step toward robust validation of on-orbit operations in microgravity-like environments.

</details>


### [302] [Evolve Vision-Language-Action Model into an Agent with On-the-fly Tool-use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14047)
*Yi Ding,Yanzhao Yu,Xili Dai,Xianbiao Qi,Peiwen Sun,Xueqian Wang,Xiangyu Yue,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ART（Agentic Robot with Tool-use）框架，将端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型与智能体工具使用相结合，通过工具注入降低动作解空间的复杂度，在减少数据依赖的同时提升泛化能力，实验表明在仿真和真实任务中比主流基线方法成功率高出20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA模型面临连续的整行动作解空间，导致复杂度过高，需要大量数据训练且泛化能力受限。作者希望通过工具使用（tool-use）来分解和简化动作解空间，从而同时提高泛化能力并降低对数据的依赖。

Method: 提出ART框架，这是一个工具注入（tool-injection）框架，可对任意VLA模型进行微调使其能够利用现成的工具模块，包括低层视觉、高层可负担性和具身增强三类工具。具体包括：构建包含30K工具使用轨迹和动作演示的数据集；设计面向长轨迹工具使用推理的训练方案；通过模块化工具利用实现更高效的训练、轻量部署和新工具的可扩展集成。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界任务（如黑暗中从新视角抓取放置）中，ART的成功率比主流基线方法高出20%，同时训练数据量远小于基线方法，验证了高泛化性和低数据依赖性的优势。

Conclusion: 基于智能体的模块化工具使用方法能够显著提升VLA系统的鲁棒性、适应性和可扩展性，为VLA系统在复杂真实世界场景中的实际部署铺平了道路。

Abstract: This paper integrates end-to-end Visual-Language-Action (VLA) models with agentic tool-use to propose Agentic Robot with Tool-use (ART). ART is a tool-injection framework that tunes any VLA model to leverage off-the-shelf tool modules for low-level vision, high-level affordance, and embodiment enhancement. Compared to vanilla VLA models with a whole continuous action solution space, ART reduces the complexity of the action solution space through tool-use, which not only improves generalizability across different tasks but also reduces data dependency. To demonstrate the advantages (high generalizability and low data dependency) of this framework, we first built a dataset of 30K tool-use trajectories and action demonstrations, which is much smaller than those used by baseline methods. We then designed a training regimen for long-trajectory tool-use reasoning in challenging environments. Experiments show that ART achieves a 20% higher success rate than mainstream baselines on simulation and real-world tasks, such as pick-and-place in the dark at novel viewpoints. Empirical results highlight the benefits of an agent-based approach: modular tool utilization enables more efficient training, lightweight deployment, and scalable integration of new tools. This design fosters robustness, adaptability, and extensibility, paving the way for the practical deployment of VLA systems in complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [303] [FlatLab: A Unified Methodology Framework and Simulation-Based Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation of Flat Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14049)
*Xingyu Zhu,Wenshuo Han,Zhouyu Wang,Yuran Wang,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong,Fan Tang,Hechang Chen,Hyung Jin Chang,Yixing Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个将扁平物体操作解耦为策略生成器与动作执行模块的统一框架，并引入FlatLab仿真基准，在多样化刚性/可变形扁平物体上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作扁平物体极具挑战性，因为这类物体处于不可抓取构型且几何与材料变化强烈。现有方法依赖启发式预操作，且多在封闭环境中评估，泛化能力有限。

Method: 将操作任务解耦为策略生成器与动作执行模块。策略生成器通过模拟数据变换和对比学习，从物体点云中学习策略中心、物体不变的表示来预测操作策略；动作执行模块基于预测策略将长视野操作分解为可复用动作原语，并动态组合以生成稳定轨迹。同时引入FlatLab仿真基准，提供高保真物理仿真、自动化多模态数据采集及标准化任务定义与评估协议。

Result: 在FlatLab上的实验表明，该方法能够有效泛化到未见物体和类别，性能优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 通过策略生成与动作执行的解耦以及统一的仿真基准，显著提升扁平物体操作的泛化能力，并公开了项目页面与代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of flat objects is challenging due to the ungraspable configurations and strong variations in object geometry and material. Existing methods rely on heuristic pre-manipulation and are often evaluated in closed settings with limited generalization. We propose a unified framework that decouples the manipulation into a strategy generator and an action execution module. The strategy generator predicts appropriate manipulation strategies from object point clouds by learning strategy-centric, object-invariant representations via simulated data transformation and contrastive learning. Conditioned on the predicted strategy, the execution module decomposes long-horizon manipulation into reusable action primitives and dynamically composes them to generate stable trajectories. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce FlatLab, a comprehensive simulation benchmark for robotic flat object manipulation. FlatLab provides high-fidelity physical simulation of diverse rigid and deformable flat objects, automated multi-modal data collection, and standardized task definitions and evaluation protocols. Experiments conducted in FlatLab demonstrate that our approach generalizes effectively to unseen objects and categories, outperforming existing baselines. The project page and the code are provided at https://flatlab-web.github.io/.

</details>


### [304] [PILOT: Privileged Imitation Learning for End-to-End Motion Planning of Autonomous UAVs under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14082)
*Qingrui Zhang,Feng Xue,Xiang Zhou,Chenghao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PILOT，一种面向部分可观测环境下视觉端到端UAV运动规划的约束感知特权模仿学习框架。它通过从最优控制专家中蒸馏规划策略，结合TCN时空感知融合模块缓解部分可观测性，并通过轨迹参数化层显式施加连续性、动力学一致性和障碍物软惩罚，在保证安全性的同时将计算开销降低80%以上，且实现了室内外零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱环境中的自主导航受到部分可观测性和动态约束的阻碍。传统的基于视觉的端到端航拍无人飞行器（UAV）运动规划方法在部分观测条件下难以获取充分的空间信息，同时计算开销大的最优控制方法虽性能优异但难以实时部署，需要在感知鲁棒性、规划安全性和计算效率之间取得平衡。

Method: PILOT框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 特权模仿学习——从计算密集型最优控制专家中蒸馏规划策略到轻量级学生网络，通过双目标损失函数在模仿学习基础上加入安全性和动力学约束正则化；(2) 时空感知融合模块——利用时序卷积网络（TCN）融合历史深度图像与里程计数据，从历史观测中推断任务相关潜在上下文，增强超出瞬时视场角（FOV）的空间感知能力而无需持久地图记忆；(3) 轨迹参数化层——将网络输出映射为结构化轨迹，在训练中显式施加连续性、动力学一致性及障碍物软惩罚，鼓励未见观测下的约束满足。

Result: 在四旋翼和固定翼飞机的仿真实验中，PILOT实现了与特权专家相当的性能，同时将计算开销降低超过80%。室内和室外环境的零样本部署实验均取得成功，验证了规划器的实际可行性和跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: PILOT通过将最优控制专家知识蒸馏到轻量级学生策略中，并结合时空感知融合与显式约束正则化，有效解决了部分可观测条件下UAV端到端运动规划的效率和鲁棒性问题。该方法在保持与专家相当性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，并展示了良好的跨域泛化能力，为视觉驱动的自主无人机导航提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in cluttered environments is hampered by partial observability and dynamic constraints. This paper presents PILOT, a constraint-aware privileged imitation learning framework for vision-based end-to-end UAV motion planning under partial observability. The framework distills planning strategies from a computationally intensive optimal control expert into a student policy regularized toward safety and dynamic requirements via a dual-objective loss function. To mitigate partial observability, a spatiotemporal perception fusion module using a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) is developed to integrate historical depth images and odometry. This module infers task-relevant latent context from historical observations, enhancing spatial awareness beyond the instantaneous FOV without maintaining persistent map memory. A trajectory parameterization layer mapping network outputs to a structured trajectory, while enabling explicit continuity, dynamic-consistency, and obstacle soft penalties during training, encouraging constraint satisfaction for unseen observations without formal guarantees. Simulations on quadrotor and fixed-wing aircraft demonstrate that PILOT achieves performance comparable to the privileged expert while reducing computational overhead by over 80\%. Successful indoor and outdoor zero-shot deployment confirms the practical feasibility and cross-domain generalization of the planner.

</details>


### [305] [AgilePE: Autonomous UAV Pursuit-Evasion via Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14135)
*Wenhao Tang,Tianyang Chen,Zhejun Cui,Boyuan An,Jiayu Chen,Ruize Zhang,Huidong Liu,Tianyue Wu,Qingmin Liao,Fei Gao,Yu Wang,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgilePE，一个基于自博弈强化学习的无人机自主追逃（pursuit-evasion）完整系统，将敏捷低层控制、竞争策略优化与sim-to-real部署集成于统一框架中。策略直接映射机载状态观测到集体推力与机体角速度（CTBR）命令，无需中间轨迹规划器。通过优先虚构自博弈（PFSP）与多样化对手池训练，策略可在真实四旋翼上零样本迁移，无需任务特定微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主追逃是无人机面临的基础挑战，需要在紧密耦合动力学和不断变化的对手行为下快速决策。传统基于规则或微分博弈的方法难以应对高维空中交互和敏捷机动，因此需要一种能够端到端学习敏捷追逃策略的新方法。

Method: AgilePE采用三层统一框架：（1）以CTBR命令实现端到端敏捷低层控制，绕过中间轨迹规划器；（2）使用优先虚构自博弈（PFSP）配合多样化对手池进行竞争性策略优化，使agent从历史策略中持续改进，同时稳定优化并减少策略振荡；（3）构建与硬件对齐的仿真管线，模拟执行器响应动力学、通信延迟和域随机化，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 学习到的策略可零样本迁移到真实四旋翼，无需任务特定调参。真实世界实验复现了仿真中观察到的追逃战术（如快速闪避和侧翼包抄），并展示了交互式双agent零样本部署能力。

Conclusion: AgilePE证明通过自博弈强化学习与硬件对齐仿真相结合，无人机可以在无需任务特定微调的情况下实现复杂的敏捷追逃行为，为高动态空中对抗任务提供了一种可部署的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous pursuit-evasion is a fundamental challenge for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), requiring rapid decision-making under tightly coupled dynamics and continuously changing opponent behaviors. Traditional rule-based or differential-game approaches often struggle with high-dimensional aerial interactions and agile maneuvering. We present AgilePE, a complete system for autonomous UAV pursuit-evasion via self-play reinforcement learning. AgilePE integrates agile low-level control, competitive policy optimization, and sim-to-real deployment in a unified framework. The policy directly maps onboard state observations to Collective Thrust and Body Rates (CTBR) commands, enabling end-to-end agile maneuvering without intermediate trajectory planners or waypoint controllers. For training, we use competitive self-play with Prioritized Fictitious Self-Play (PFSP) and a diversified opponent pool, enabling agents to improve against historical policies while stabilizing optimization and reducing policy oscillation. This process leads to the emergence of sophisticated pursuit and evasion strategies. For real-world deployment, we develop a hardware-aligned simulation pipeline that models actuator-response dynamics, communication latency, and domain randomization. The learned policies transfer zero-shot to real quadrotors without task-specific tuning. Real-world experiments reproduce pursuit-evasion tactics observed in simulation, including rapid dodging and flanking, and demonstrate interactive two-agent zero-shot deployment.

</details>


### [306] [OccPlanner: Goal-Aware Occupancy-Conditioned Diffusion Planner for Pixel-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14160)
*Binling Huang,Nianjin Ye,Xi Yang,Liang Hu,Zhou Huang,Shuang Wei,Longrui Yang,Yanchi Chen,Lanpeng Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OccPlanner，一种目标感知的占用条件扩散规划器，将像素级导航目标在自我中心度量空间中接地，并顺序地将目标表示与时间视觉上下文和学习的局部3D占用特征条件化。同时引入L3ROcc，通过几何重建和基于射线的可见性推理，将单目RGB导航视频转换为机器人中心局部3D占用标注。在闭环仿真中，5-8米设置下平均成功率从20.81%提升至71.55%（相对NavDP），并在Unitree Go2上进行真实世界开环实验验证了sim-to-real迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级目标导航（pixel-goal navigation）中的目标像素缺少度量深度和可通行性信息，导致3D目标接地和无碰撞连续规划具有挑战性。现有方法如NavDP在该任务上成功率较低（平均20.81%），亟需一种能够将2D像素目标转换到3D度量空间并生成无碰撞轨迹的规划方法。

Method: OccPlanner包含两大部分：(1) 目标感知的占用条件扩散规划器——将像素目标在自我中心度量空间中接地，并顺序地（sequentially）将目标表示与时间视觉上下文和学习的局部3D占用特征进行条件化，以生成无碰撞连续轨迹；(2) L3ROcc数据生成管线——通过几何重建和射线可见性推理，将大规模单目RGB导航视频自动转换为机器人中心局部3D占用标注，提供可扩展的占用监督信号。

Result: 在InternScenes的四个未见场景类别和两种目标距离设置上进行闭环评估。在5-8米设置下，OccPlanner将平均成功率从NavDP的20.81%提升到71.55%，在cluttered-easy和cluttered-hard场景中分别达到86.20%和84.92%。此外，在Unitree Go2机器人上进行的真实世界开环实验提供了sim-to-real迁移和利用L3ROcc生成监督进行适应的初步证据。

Conclusion: 通过对目标感知的占用条件化扩散规划与大规模自动生成3D占用标注相结合，OccPlanner显著提升了像素级目标导航的成功率和安全性，并展示了从仿真到真实世界的迁移潜力。

Abstract: Pixel-goal navigation specifies targets directly in the agent's camera view, but a target pixel provides neither metric depth nor traversability, making 3D goal grounding and collision-free continuous planning challenging. We present OccPlanner, a goal-aware occupancy-conditioned diffusion planner that grounds pixel goals in egocentric metric space and sequentially conditions the goal representation on temporal visual context and learned local 3D occupancy features. To provide occupancy supervision at scale, we introduce L3ROcc, which converts monocular RGB navigation videos into robot-centric local 3D occupancy annotations through geometric reconstruction and ray-based visibility reasoning. We train OccPlanner on InternData-N1 and evaluate it in closed-loop simulation across four unseen scene categories from InternScenes and two goal-distance ranges. In the 5-8 m setting, OccPlanner increases the average success rate (SR) over NavDP from 20.81% to 71.55% across the four categories, reaching 86.20% and 84.92% in cluttered-easy and cluttered-hard scenes, respectively. Real-world open-loop experiments on a Unitree Go2 further provide initial evidence of sim-to-real transfer and adaptation with L3ROcc-generated supervision.

</details>


### [307] [MMUSV-Sim: A Perception-Oriented Simulation and Data-Generation Platform for Multi-USV Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14207)
*Ziao Li,Jianxiong Ye,Biao Tang,Leping Zhang,Kun Zuo,Siyu Huang,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MMUSV-Sim，一个基于Unreal Engine 5和Project AirSim的多无人水面艇协同感知仿真与数据生成平台，支持岛屿、开阔海域和港口环境，可配置天气、时间和波浪条件，并提供多模态数据采集（RGB、深度、语义、LiDAR、雷达）及多智能体共享世界状态标注导出。实验验证了波浪设置对船体运动的正确影响，并证明基于LiDAR的协同BEV船舰检测中，早期融合相比单艇AP@0.5提升显著（72.74 vs 45.54）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人水面艇协同感知可融合互补观测，扩展海上目标感知范围，但大规模系统研发缺乏可配置多USV场景、多模态采集和共享标注的统一工作流，因此需要专门的感知仿真与数据生成平台。

Method: 基于Unreal Engine 5和Project AirSim构建MMUSV-Sim仿真平台，提供岛屿、开阔海域、港口三种环境，支持可配置的天气、昼夜和波浪条件，包含多样船舶资产库和基于样条曲线的多船运动控制。平台在多个USV上同步采集RGB、深度、语义、LiDAR和雷达数据，并捕获统一世界状态用于逐智能体标注导出。

Result: 实验验证了配置的波浪设置能在船体升沉、横摇和纵摇上产生预期运动变化，并评估了投影标注与语义渲染间的几何一致性。在生成的多USV数据集上进行基于LiDAR的协同BEV船舰检测实验，早期融合达到AP@0.5为72.74，而单USV仅为45.54。

Conclusion: MMUSV-Sim为多USV协同感知研究提供了统一的仿真和数据生成平台，通过协同融合显著提升了BEV目标检测性能，验证了该平台在支持大规模多USV感知系统开发方面的有效性。

Abstract: Cooperative perception among multiple unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) combines complementary observations to extend maritime target sensing beyond the view range and field of a single platform. Developing such systems at scale calls for a unified workflow for configurable multi-USV scenarios, multimodal acquisition, and shared annotations. We present MMUSV-Sim, a perception-oriented maritime simulation and data-generation platform built on Unreal Engine 5 and Project AirSim. It provides island, open-sea, and port environments; configurable weather, time of day, and wave conditions; a diverse vessel asset library; and spline-based multi-vessel motion. MMUSV-Sim acquires RGB, depth, semantic, LiDAR, and radar observations across multiple USVs and captures a common world state for per-agent annotation export. Experiments verify that the configured wave settings produce the intended changes in vessel heave, roll, and pitch, and evaluate the geometric consistency between projected annotations and semantic renderings. In LiDAR-based cooperative BEV vessel detection experiments on the generated multi-USV dataset, Early Fusion achieves an AP@0.5 of 72.74, compared with 45.54 using a single USV.

</details>


### [308] [Vibration Suppression in Collaborative Flexible Payload Manipulation Using Passive Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14244)
*Alaa Abderrahim,Antonio Rosales,Ferdinando Milella,Markku Suomalainen,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主从机器人协同操作的柔性载荷振动抑制策略，通过领导者机器人发出整形速度指令引导运动，跟随者机器人利用导纳控制器响应外部力，实现对大型柔性结构在运动过程中的横向振动抑制。系统采用等效质量-弹簧-阻尼模型建模，证明了系统能量的被动耗散，并通过数值仿真和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型重型结构在运动过程中会产生振动，给精确操作带来巨大挑战。在先进项目中（如未来聚变能反应堆（托卡马克）的远程维护），这类结构的操控被定义为关键任务，亟需有效的振动抑制控制策略。

Method: 采用双工业机械臂的主从（leader-follower）协同操作方案：领导者机器人通过整形速度指令引导运动路径，跟随者机器人通过导纳控制器对领导者施加在载荷上的估计外部力做出柔顺响应。系统动力学用等效质量-弹簧-阻尼模型描述，并针对异构机器人规格进行了适配。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提方法的正确性，实验结果表明该方法能够有效抑制柔性载荷在运动过程中的横向振动，且与现有方法相比，能够协同操作更重、更大的柔性物体。

Conclusion: 证明了在适当的导纳参数下系统总能量可被动耗散，并提供了稳定性证明；该方法解决了异构机器人协同操作重型柔性物体的振动抑制问题，为复杂环境（如托卡马克远程维护）中的精准操控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In large and heavy structures, vibrations arise during motion, posing significant challenges for precise manipulation. To accomplish the desired motion, control algorithms must effectively suppress these structural vibrations. In cutting edge projects, such as remote maintenance of future fusion energy reactors (tokamaks), the manipulation of this type of structure is defined as a crucial task. This paper presents a control strategy to suppress transverse vibrations in flexible payloads during motion using a collaborative payload manipulation approach. Two different industrial robot arms are arranged in a leader follower configuration for the manipulation strategy. The leader robot guides the motion with shaped velocity commands, while the follower robot ensures compliance with the estimated external forces applied by the leader on the payload through an admittance controller. Unlike existing methods, the proposed approach enables collaborative manipulation of heavier and larger flexible objects, addressing additional challenges such as vibration suppression and heterogeneous robot specifications. The dynamics of the leader follower payload system are modeled using an equivalent mass spring damper model, and it is shown that, with appropriate admittance parameters, the total energy of the system is passively dissipated. A stability proof is also provided. Numerical simulations validate the proposed method, and experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [309] [Accelerating Large-scale Bundle Adjustment for LiDAR Mapping via Parallel Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14266)
*Yixi Cai,Rundong Li,Yuhan Xie,Qingwen Zhang,Patric Jensfelt,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个全并行计算框架来加速大规模建图中的LiDAR束调整，包含三项关键技术：自适应异步数据加载、自底向上的体素化平面特征提取方法、以及基于majorization-minimization公式的并行优化加速。在多种计算平台上实现了高达十倍的效率提升，同时保持与最先进方法相当的建图精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR束调整在大规模建图中广泛用于构建全局一致的点云地图，但现有方法在处理大规模数据时计算效率低下，且受限于GPU显存容量，缺少面向大规模LiDAR束调整的高效并行计算框架。

Method: 提出三项关键技术：(1) 自适应异步数据加载策略，用于在显存受限的GPU上高效处理大规模点云数据；(2) 新颖的自底向上体素化方法提取平面特征，实现全并行的预处理；(3) 基于majorization-minimization公式，并行加速优化中的残差、Jacobian和Hessian矩阵计算，并设计并行增量求解器。同时提供时间复杂度理论分析。

Result: 在多种大规模公开数据集和不同计算平台上进行广泛基准测试，计算效率提升最高达十倍，同时建图精度与最先进方法相当。代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: 所提出的全并行LiDAR束调整框架在保证精度的前提下显著提升了计算效率，验证了方法的鲁棒性和适应性，为大规模点云建图提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: LiDAR bundle adjustment is widely utilized in mapping to construct globally consistent point cloud maps. In this paper, we propose the first fully parallel computing framework to accelerate LiDAR bundle adjustment for large-scale mapping, incorporating three key techniques. First, we design an adaptive, asynchronous data loading strategy to efficiently process large-scale point cloud datasets on memory-constrained GPUs. Secondly, we present a novel bottom-up voxelization method for extracting planar features, enabling fully parallelized pre-processing. Thirdly, we build upon a majorization-minimization formulation to accelerate compute-intensive tasks in the optimization via parallel computation, including the computation of residuals, Jacobian and Hessian matrices, and a parallel increment solver. To support our design, we provide both theoretical and experimental analysis of the time complexity of our approach. Extensive benchmarking on large-scale public datasets across various computational platforms validates the robustness and adaptability of our approach, achieving up to a tenfold improvement in computational efficiency while preserving mapping accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art methods. To benefit future research, the implementation code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [310] [PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5: A Toolkit for Robot Process Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14284)
*Yuyang Liu,Yanqing Shen,Ruike Chen,Jifan Zhao,Yuxuan Tian,Yichi Zhang,Tianfeng Long,Zixuan Yin,Yipu Wang,Ziheng Qin,Wenxing Tan,Yang Shi,Mingyu Cao,Runze Xiao,Ziqi Wang,Zhixin Yin,Shiwei Chu,Yi-Fan Zhang,Yao Mu,Yuheng Ji,Yihao Wang,Jun Yan,Zhongyuan Wang,Pengwei Wang,Xiaolong Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5，一个机器人过程评估工具包，通过将滚动视频转换为密集进度曲线来提取多种细粒度指标，并引入三个新指标（失败侧进展、回撤后恢复、成功侧执行质量），同时提出RoboPulse++评估过程奖励模型的可靠性，并发布包含基准、指标实现和可视化工具的评估套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人评估严重依赖二元成功率和基于规则的流程评分，无法深入理解具身模型的真实能力。细粒度的过程评估对于理解具身模型至关重要，需要超越简单的成败判断，提供更丰富、更准确的评估维度。

Method: 1) 将滚动视频转化为密集进度曲线；2) 在v1.0基础上引入三个新指标：失败侧进展、回撤后恢复、成功侧执行质量；3) 提出RoboPulse++平台评估过程奖励模型的可靠性；4) 发布包含基准、度量实现和可视化工具的用户友好评估套件。

Result: 基于多个基准的滚动视频对具身模型进行了全面评估，提供了细粒度度量结果和关键发现，并推出了RoboPulse++为评估者提供更准确的过程奖励模型测试平台。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区重新思考机器人评估方式，建立透明、程序化、可复现的评估作为下一代具身智能的基础。

Abstract: Fine-grained robotic evaluation matters for understanding embodied models, going beyond binary success rates and rule-based process scores. We present PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5, a toolkit for robot process assessment that turns rollout videos into dense progress curves and derives multiple fine metrics. PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5 introduces three metrics, building on version 1.0, that characterize failure-side progress, post-drawdown recovery, and success-side execution quality, helping users understand embodied model capability. Based on the rollout videos from benchmarks, we perform a comprehensive assessment of the embodied models, providing some fine-grained metric results and key findings. We further introduce RoboPulse++ to evaluate the reliability of process reward models (PRM), providing evaluators with a more accurate testing platform. Moreover, we release a user-friendly assessment suite, including the benchmark, metric implementation, and visualization tools, to support reproducible manipulation process evaluation. We call on the community to rethink how robots are evaluated and establish transparent, procedural, and reproducible assessment as a foundation for the next generation of embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [311] [CORAL: Curriculum-Optimized Reward Adaptation for LiDAR-Based Goal-Directed Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14332)
*Anisa Saleem,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORAL提出了一种双管齐下的训练方案——五阶段课程学习与阶段感知奖励相结合，用于自动驾驶长时程目标导航。通过多流actor-critic网络和PPO在CARLA中训练，在最具挑战性的长路线全约束场景下达到100%成功率，远超基线（5%/10%），并实现在七个未见城镇的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在自动驾驶城市导航中具有潜力，但长时程目标导向导航要求策略同时习得多项竞争性行为（到达遥远目标、跟踪路线、避障、遵守信号），而固定目标函数无法确定这些行为的学习顺序，导致难以有效训练。

Method: CORAL同时推进两个调度机制：（1）五阶段课程，逐步延长路线并收紧行为约束；（2）阶段感知奖励，其组件权重随任务难度从任务进度转向路线跟随、安全、平滑性和规则合规。策略采用多流actor-critic网络，在CARLA中用PPO训练，输入为99维紧凑状态（极坐标LiDAR直方图、车辆遥测数据、自车框架路线几何和交通规则指示器），无需点云编码器或鸟瞰图栅格化。

Result: 在最长的路线和完整行为约束下，CORAL在全部20个评估回合中均成功到达目标（100%），而两个PPO基线仅分别达到5%和10%。因子消融实验表明单一调度均无法匹敌组合效果：移除任一调度会降低成功率和路线完成度，同时禁用则成功率降至55%。单一城镇训练后，零样本迁移至七个未见城镇，在100-150m路线上成功率达68-98%，平均横向偏差低于0.35m。

Conclusion: 课程学习与阶段感知奖励的协同组合是解决长时程自动驾驶导航中多行为竞争学习的关键，CORAL在保持输入表示紧凑高效的同时实现了高成功率和良好的泛化能力，证明了无需复杂感知编码即可实现可靠的城市自动驾驶导航。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is promising for autonomous urban driving, but long-horizon goal-directed navigation asks a policy to acquire several competing behaviors at once--reaching a distant goal, tracking a route, avoiding obstacles, obeying signals--and a fixed objective gives no order in which to learn them. This paper presents CORAL, which advances two schedules together: a five-stage curriculum that progressively lengthens routes and tightens behavioral constraints, and a stage-aware reward whose component weights shift emphasis from mission progress toward route following, safety, smoothness, and rule compliance as the task hardens. The policy is a multi-stream actor-critic network trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in CARLA on a compact 99-dimensional state pairing a polar LiDAR histogram with vehicle telemetry, ego-frame route geometry, and traffic-rule indicators--no point-cloud encoder, no bird's-eye-view rasterization. Against two PPO baselines under an identical protocol, CORAL reaches the goal in all twenty evaluation episodes on the longest routes under the full set of behavioral constraints, where the baselines reach 5% and 10%; a factorial ablation shows that neither schedule alone matches their combination: removing either lowers both success and route completion, and disabling both drops success to 55%. Trained in one town, the policy transfers zero-shot to seven unseen towns, succeeding in 68-98% of episodes on routes of the same 100-150 m length, with mean lateral deviation below 0.35 m.

</details>


### [312] [Reflex: Enabling Fast and Predictive Vision-Language-Action Models for Reaction-Critical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14379)
*Yuxuan Chen,Wanruo Zhang,Xiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReflexBench基准和ReflexVLA模型，填补了机器人操作中动态交互场景评估的空白。ReflexBench包含六个动态任务，并设计了将模拟器步进与机器人控制解耦、支持同步/异步推理和可配置延迟的评估框架。ReflexVLA通过潜在未来预测和多帧时间融合增强时序推理，并利用批量视觉编码和CUDA图重放降低部署延迟，无需大规模机器人数据预训练即可提升动态操作性能，同时保持静态基准上的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型（视觉-语言-动作模型）在机器人操作中表现良好，但现有基准主要评估静态操作任务的泛化能力，忽略了动态交互场景（如对快速变化的物体或环境做出反应性操作）。这一缺口导致VLA模型在需要反应式操控的实际场景中缺乏系统评估基准和高效模型。

Method: 1) 提出ReflexBench基准：包含六个动态操作任务；2) 引入评估框架：将模拟器步进与机器人控制解耦，支持同步和异步推理下的可配置延迟；3) 提出ReflexVLA模型：通过潜在未来预测增强时序推理，在视觉骨干中实现多帧时间融合，采用批量视觉编码和CUDA Graph重放降低部署延迟。

Result: 实验表明ReflexVLA在动态操作任务上持续提升性能，同时在标准静态操作基准上保持接近的准确性；真实世界实验进一步验证了实际部署条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: ReflexBench为反应式操作提供了系统化评估工具，ReflexVLA证明了无需大规模机器人数据预训练即可实现高效反应式操作的可能性，为VLA模型在动态场景中的进一步发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently achieved promising performance in robotic manipulation. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate generalization on static manipulation tasks and largely overlook dynamic interaction scenarios. To address this gap, we present ReflexBench, a benchmark for reaction-critical manipulation. ReflexBench contains six dynamic tasks and introduces an evaluation framework that decouples simulator stepping from robot control while supporting configurable latency under synchronous and asynchronous inference. Building upon ReflexBench, we propose ReflexVLA, an efficient VLA model designed for reaction-critical manipulation without large-scale robot-data pretraining. ReflexVLA enhances temporal reasoning through latent future prediction and multi-frame temporal fusion within the vision backbone, while reducing deployment latency through batched visual encoding and CUDA Graph replay. Experiments show that ReflexVLA consistently improves dynamic manipulation performance while maintaining competitive accuracy on standard static manipulation benchmarks, and real-world experiments further demonstrate its effectiveness under practical deployment conditions. Project website: https://reflexvla.github.io

</details>


### [313] [Effect of Twisted-Yarn Architecture on Pressure and Proximity Sensing Characteristics of Textile Capacitive Sensors for Robotic Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14406)
*Ishtia Zahir,Eslam Saleh,Maryam Rezayati,Güunter Grabher,Gaffar Hossain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于银涂层纱线结合PDMS涂覆并组装成一、二、四层加捻结构的纺织电容传感平台，系统研究了纱线层级架构对电容传感性能的影响，实现了压力与近距传感的可调谐设计，并验证了其在空间映射和机器人集成方面的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纺织集成电容传感器虽然具有柔性和共形触觉传感的优势，但纱线层级架构对电容传感特性的影响尚未被充分量化，缺乏系统性的架构-性能关系研究。

Method: 采用银涂层纱线涂覆PDMS，构建一、二、四层加捻构型；通过局部单纤维接触面积计算压力，系统研究有效电极重叠面积与纤维间分离对电容响应的影响；测试了四层结构的灵敏度、迟滞、热漂移、循环稳定性，并进行了4×4传感阵列空间映射和机械臂集成实验。

Result: 层数增加显著提升力学性能（断裂伸长率从37.5%提至85.0%，最大载荷从23.3提至89.7 N）；四层传感器在100 kHz下灵敏度达0.1331 MPa^-1，表现出低迟滞、低热漂移（25-90°C）和15000次循环稳定性；近距检测范围随层数增加而减小（60、50、40 mm），呈现架构依赖的灵敏度-范围权衡；4×4阵列实现空间触觉映射，机械臂集成系统端到端延迟为403 ms。

Conclusion: 纱线架构可作为纺织集成电容传感系统的可调谐设计参数，直接决定压力与近距传感的测量特性，为柔性可穿戴和机器人触觉传感提供了新的设计维度。

Abstract: Textile-integrated capacitive sensors offer flexible and conformable tactile sensing for wearable electronics and human-robot interaction; however, the influence of yarn-level architecture on capacitive transduction characteristics remains insufficiently quantified. This work presents a textile capacitive sensing platform based on silver-coated yarns coated with polydimethylsiloxane and assembled into one-, two-, and four-layer twisted configurations. The influence of effective electrode overlap area and inter-fiber separation on the capacitive response is systematically investigated, enabling architecture-dependent tuning of pressure and proximity sensing characteristics. Pressure was calculated using the localized single-fiber contact area, corresponding to stresses of 0.4-3.9 MPa. Increasing the layer number improved mechanical strength and sensing performance: elongation at break increased from 37.5% to 62.5% and 85.0%, while the maximum load increased from 23.3 to 42.7 and 89.7 N. Sensitivity increased with layer number and frequency, reaching 0.1331 MPa$^{-1}$ for the four-layer sensor at 100 kHz. The four-layer configuration also exhibited low hysteresis, minimal thermal drift from 25 to 90 $^\circ$C, and stable operation over 15,000 cycles. Proximity detection ranges of 60, 50, and 40 mm were obtained for the one-, two-, and four-layer sensors, respectively, revealing an architecture-dependent sensitivity-range trade-off. A 4$\times$4 textile sensing array enabled spatial contact mapping, while robotic-arm integration demonstrated real-time touch and proximity detection with an end-to-end robotic system latency (from detection to robot reaction) of 403 ms. The results establish yarn architecture as a tunable design parameter governing the measurement characteristics of textile-integrated capacitive sensing systems.

</details>


### [314] [Control-Informed Constraint Adaptation in Minimum-Time Trajectory Planning for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14448)
*Ann-Kathrin Schwehn,Alexander Langmann,Mattia Piccinini,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种控制信息感知的在线轨迹规划框架，通过运行时测量系统跟踪偏差并动态调整空间约束，迭代扩展自由空间规划区域，在不增加计算负担的情况下实现时间最优规划，仿真实验显示圈速提升1.8秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车在车辆动力极限附近运行时，微小控制误差会导致安全关键行为和性能损失。传统轨迹规划器假设完美跟踪，对执行误差视而不见，因此只能采用保守的空间裕度来保证安全，导致大量可用的赛道空间未被利用。

Method: 提出控制信息感知的在线轨迹规划框架：在运行时测量系统性的跟踪偏差，据此动态调整空间赛道约束，迭代扩展自由空间规划区域，同时保持时间最优性并补偿累积的执行误差。

Result: 在高保真闭环仿真环境中验证，圈速降低1.8秒，且未增加计算负担，中位运行时间维持在25毫秒。

Conclusion: 将控制引起的偏差反馈到规划层，可以解锁模块化架构此前无法实现的性能，使自动驾驶车辆能够系统地利用赛道极限。这一发现表明控制与规划之间的协同反馈是提升自动驾驶赛车性能的关键路径。

Abstract: Autonomous racecars operate at the limits of vehicle dynamics, where small control errors translate into safety-critical behavior and lost performance. Trajectory planners assume perfect tracking and remain blind to execution errors. To guarantee safety, trajectory planners therefore restrict themselves to conservative spatial margins, leaving usable track space untapped. To overcome these issues, we introduce a control-informed online trajectory planning framework that learns from its own execution errors. By measuring systematic tracking deviations during runtime, we dynamically adapt spatial track constraints and iteratively expand the free-space planning area. The planner remains time-optimal while compensating for accumulated execution errors. This method was analyzed in a high-fidelity closed-loop simulation environment with autonomous racecars. The results demonstrate that our approach reduces lap time by 1.8\,s without increasing computational burden, maintaining a median runtime of 25 ms. Our finding indicates that feeding control-induced deviations back into the planning layer unlocks performance previously inaccessible to modular architectures and enables autonomous vehicles to exploit track limits systematically.

</details>


### [315] [THRIVE: Therapeutic Humanoid Robot In Virtual Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14462)
*Jin Xu,Yu-Ping Chen,Ayanna Howard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了THRIVE（虚拟环境中的治疗性人形机器人），一个居家康复平台，集成了虚拟现实上肢康复游戏、基于摄像头的实时运动追踪系统和社交互动机器人治疗师。该系统通过可定制的弹跳、击打、抓取等游戏任务，针对上肢运动障碍儿童的功能性伸展、抓握和物体操控动作提供治疗，并使用机器人治疗师（物理机器人或远程虚拟代理）提供自适应动态反馈。其机器人无关的模块化设计使系统经济、可扩展且易于家庭长期使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上肢运动障碍儿童需要持续、任务特异性的练习来改善功能，但传统居家康复缺乏趣味性和持续动力。需要一个低成本、可扩展且能提供一致激励的居家康复解决方案。

Method: THRIVE采用模块化架构，将治疗性游戏与机器人载体解耦。系统包含三部分：VR上肢康复游戏套件（针对功能性伸展、抓握和操控，通过弹跳、击打、抓取等可定制任务实现）、基于摄像头的实时运动追踪系统（捕获儿童运动学表现）、以及可部署为物理机器人或远程虚拟代理的社交互动机器人治疗师（提供自适应动态反馈以激励和引导运动）。

Result: 该平台实现了机器人无关的模块化设计，支持多种机器人实体和不同机器人，使系统具备经济性、可扩展性和家庭环境适配性，为儿童提供更持续、更有吸引力的上肢治疗途径。

Conclusion: THRIVE通过游戏化VR康复与社交机器人反馈的整合，提供了一个实用且可扩展的居家上肢康复方案，有望提高治疗的一致性和参与度，改善运动障碍儿童的治疗效果。

Abstract: This paper presents THRIVE (Therapeutic Humanoid Robot In Virtual Environment), an at-home rehabilitation platform that integrates a suite of virtual-reality upper-body rehabilitation games, a real-time camera-based motion-tracking system, and a socially interactive robot therapist. The system is designed for therapy and intervention in children with upper-limb motor impairments, which can be improved through consistent, task-specific practice. THRIVE features a set of newly designed, engaging games that target functional reaching, grasping, and object-manipulation movements through customizable popping, hitting, catching, and grabbing tasks, while the camera-based tracking system captures the child's kinematic performance during play. A robot therapist - deployable either as a physical robotic coach or as a remote-presence virtual agent - delivers adaptive, dynamic feedback to motivate the child and guide their movements toward therapeutic goals. THRIVE decouples the therapeutic games from the robot embodiment, extending the platform to support various embodiments and different robots within one modular system. This robot-agnostic design makes THRIVE affordable, scalable, and readily adaptable for sustained use in the home, offering a practical pathway to more consistent and engaging upper-limb therapy for children with motor function impairments.

</details>


### [316] [Expected Free Energy-based Informative Path Planning for Robotic Mars Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14466)
*Ajith Anil Meera,Pablo Lanillos,Wouter Kouw*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将主动推理中的期望自由能（EFE）作为统一目标函数，用于预算约束下的机器人信息性路径规划。在硬路径长度约束下，代理在保持高斯过程信念的同时规划连续轨迹以最小化EFE，从而同时构建准确的信息地图并定位高价值区域，实验表明其优于信息论基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在未知环境（如火星找水）中探索时面临双重需求：构建准确的信息地图，同时快速找到最大价值区域，且需考虑每米行进和每次测量的成本。经典的信息搜索标准和奖励搜索标准各自只解决其中一个目标，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出使用主动推理中的期望自由能（EFE）作为统一的目标函数，将其应用于预算约束下的机器人信息性路径规划。代理在信息场上维护高斯过程信念，在硬路径长度约束下规划连续轨迹以最小化期望自由能，从而实现信息收集与价值寻找的统一优化。

Result: 多次实验实现结果表明，基于EFE的规划能够同时生成准确的后验地图并定位最高价值区域，在相同设置下优于信息论基线方法。

Conclusion: 这种统一、易于调参的原则性信息收集策略能够促进自主部署，同时强制执行效率和资源约束，为机器人探索提供了一种统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: An autonomous robot efficiently exploring an unknown environment, such as looking for water sources on Mars, faces two simultaneous demands: building an accurate information map while quickly finding the regions of greatest value, and paying for every meter of travel and the cost of every measurement it takes. Classical information-seeking and reward-seeking criteria address only one of these objectives at a time. Here, we propose Expected Free Energy (EFE), the principled action-selection objective from active inference, as a unifying criterion for budgeted robotic informative path planning. Maintaining a Gaussian-process belief over the information field, our agent plans continuous trajectories that minimize expected free energy under hard path-length constraints. The results from multiple realizations show that EFE-based planning yields accurate posterior maps and locates the highest-value regions simultaneously, outperforming information-theoretic baselines under the same settings. In robotic exploration, these unified, easy-to-tune principled information-gathering strategies facilitate autonomous deployment while enforcing efficiency and resource constraints.

</details>


### [317] [Ensuring Safe Physical AI in Urban Mobility via Hazard-Informed Synthesized Envelopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14481)
*Alexei Odinokov,Rostislav Yavorskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，通过"危险知悉的安全包络"（hazard-informed safety envelopes）桥接系统化危险分析与运行时执行，实现异构机器人在复杂城市场景中的安全移动。该框架将安全从静态约束转变为涵盖符号、空间和动态世界模型的跨层安全变换过程，并与物理AI运行时工具链自然对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人系统在多样城市场景中部署时，安全维持仍是关键挑战，尤其是在复杂人机交互环境中。现有方法通常将安全视为隔离在单个软件模块内的静态约束，缺乏系统化、跨层级的危险分析与运行时保障的统一机制。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，核心思想是使用"危险知悉的安全包络"来桥接系统化危险分析与运行时执行。方法包括：(1) 跨层安全变换过程，涵盖符号世界模型、空间世界模型和动态世界模型三个层面；(2) 安全包络作为统一表示，与物理AI运行时工具链（runtime harnesses）接口对接，以保障城市移动安全。

Result: 本文展示了该安全表示能够自然地与物理AI运行时工具链交互，从而保证异构机器人在城市环境中的安全移动，实现了从系统级危险分析到运行时执行的无缝衔接。

Conclusion: 该方法为异构机器人城市部署提供了一种系统化、跨层次的安全保障范式，打破传统静态安全约束的局限，使安全分析和运行时执行形成闭环，为复杂人机交互环境下的安全移动提供了可扩展的框架基础。

Abstract: As heterogeneous robotic systems deploy across diverse urban zones, maintaining safety amid complex human-robot interactions remains a critical challenge. We present a unified framework that bridges systematic hazard analysis and runtime enforcement using hazard-informed safety envelopes. Rather than treating safety as a static constraint isolated within individual software modules, we introduce a cross-layer safety transformation process spanning symbolic, spatial, and dynamic world models. We show how this representation naturally interfaces with physical AI runtime harnesses to guarantee safe urban mobility.

</details>


### [318] [Spatiotemporal Tube-Based Safety-Certificate for Autonomous Navigation of Articulated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14531)
*Mohd. Faizuddin Faruqui,Ratnangshu Das,Ravi Kumar L,Pushpak Jagtap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空管道的规划方法，用于铰接式车辆（如牵引半挂车、卡车拖车、牵引AGV和公路列车）的自主导航。该方法利用铰接单元的动力学和摆振约束，为执行驱动的牵引车规划安全管道路径，并通过对管道施加允许修正量来提供路线安全认证，确保所有拖挂车厢始终保持在道路走廊内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接式车辆是货运的主力，但其自主导航极具挑战性。由于物理特性和运动约束，这些车辆在狭窄路线上操纵困难，需要一种能够确保拖挂车厢不出界的安全规划方法。

Method: 采用时空管道（spatiotemporal tube）方法为执行驱动的牵引车规划路径。该规划利用铰接单元的运动学特性和摆振（sway）约束，构建安全的管道空间；并通过允许修正量（permissible corrections）对时空管道进行修正，为目标车辆提供路线安全认证。

Result: 在一条复杂路线上的卡车-拖车导航仿真中验证了所提出的规划方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的时空管道规划方法能够为铰接式车辆提供认证的安全路径，确保拖挂系列车厢始终保持在道路走廊范围内，适用于多种类型的铰接式货运车辆。

Abstract: Articulated vehicles are the workhorses of freight transportation, and their autonomous navigation is challenging. Their physical characteristics and motion constraints pose significant challenges in manoeuvring these vehicles on narrow routes. This paper presents a spatiotemporal tube-based approach to plan autonomous navigation of vehicles like tractor semi-trailers, truck/ tractor trailers, towing Automated Guided Vehicles (AGVs), and road trains. This planning approach provides a certified path plan for the truck or tractor, ensuring that the towed series of trailers always remains within the road corridor, limited by permissible corrections. The planning leverages the kinematics of the linked elements along with sway constraints to arrive at a safe tube for the actuated prime mover. We modify the spatiotemporal tube using permissible corrections to provide a route safety certificate to the vehicle for the given route. The proposed planning method is verified on a truck-trailer navigation simulation for a complex route.

</details>
